   e-ṭu: iruḷattukkoṇṭāṉ iruḷiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum
   cāriyai varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattum oṭaḷṭuka. iruḷattu ñāṉṟāṉ
   nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ iruḷiṉñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉa varum. (107)
   -----------
   "puḷ" "vaḷ" eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ
   403. puḷḷum vaḷḷun toḻiṟpeya riyala.
   ituvum eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu. tiripum iyalpum
   vilakkit toḻiṟpeyarviti vakuttaliṉ.
   i-ḷ: puḷḷumvaḷḷum-puḷḷeṉṉuñ collum vaḷḷeṉṉuñ collum iruvaḻik kaṇṇum,
   toḻiṟpeyar iyala-ñakāra īṟṟut toḻiṟpeyar pōla vaṉkaṇattuukaramum
   valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭaikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu
   muṭiyum; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: puḷḷukkaṭitu vaḷḷukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu
   māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, puḷḷukkaṭumai vaḷḷukkaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai
   perumai ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmai eṉavum varum.
   itaṉait "toḻiṟ peyarellām" (eḻu-401) eṉpataṉpiṉ vaiyātataṉāl iruvaḻiyum
   vēṟṟumaittiripu eyti muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. puṭkaṭitu vaṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu
   peritu eṉavum, puṭkaṭumai vaṭkaṭamai ciṟumai tīmai perumai eṉavum,
   puṇñāṉṟatu nīṭatu māṇṭatu eṉavum, puṇñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum varum.
   puḷḷu valitu puḷvalitu puḷḷuvaṉmai puḷvaṉmai eṉa vakarattiṉ muṉṉar
   ukaram peṟṟum peṟātum varutaliṉ "niṉṟacoṉmu ṉiyalpākum" (eḻu-144)
   eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭiyāmai uṇarka. itu vaḷḷiṟkum okkum. (108)
   ----------
   "makkaḷ" eṉṉuñ col
   404. makka ḷeṉṉum peyarcco liṟuti
   takkavaḻi aṟintu valittalu murittē
   iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu. "uyirīṟākiya uyar
   tiṇaippeyar" (eḻu-153) eṉpataṉuṭ kūṟiya iyalpu vilakkit tiripu
   vakuttaliṉ.
   i-ḷ: makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarc col iṟuti-makkaḷeṉṉum peyarc colliṟuti
   iyalpēyaṉṟi, takkavaḻi aṟintu valittalum urittu-takka iṭam aṟintu
   valloṟṟākat tirintu muṭitalum urittu; e-ṟu
   takkavaḻiyeṉṟār perumpāṉmai makkaḷuṭampu uyirnīṅkik kiṭanta kālattiṉ
   aḵtu immuṭipupeṟum eṉṟaṟku.
   e-ṭu: makkaṭkai cevi talai puṟam. "ikkiṭantatu makkaṭṭalai" eṉpataṉāṉ
   avvāṟātal koḷka. makkaḷ kai cevi talai puṟam eṉat tiriyātu niṉṟatu
   uyiruṇmai peṟṟu
   iṉic ciṟupāṉmai makkaṭ paṇpu makkaṭcuṭṭu eṉavum varum (109)
   ----------

                            8.3. puṟaṉaṭai

   ivvayaliṉ puṟaṉaṭai
   405. uṇarak kūṟiya puṇariyal maruṅkiṟ
   kaṇṭucayaṟ kuriyavai kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē
   iḵtu ivvōttiṉkaṇ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām ituvē
   ōttākak koṇṭu cāriyai peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuc cāriyaiyum eḻuttuop
   peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku eḻuttuṅ koṭuttu muṭittukkoḷka eṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uṇarak kūṟiya puṇariyaṉ maruṅkiṉ-uṇarak kūṟappaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu
   varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum iyalpiṭattu, kaṇṭu ceyaṟku uriyavai-mēl muṭitta
   muṭipaṉṟi vaḻakkiṉuṭ kaṇṭu muṭittaṟku uriyavai tōṉṟiyavaḻi, kaṇṇiṉar
   koḷal-avaṟṟaiyuṅ karutikkoṇṭu ēṟṟavāṟē muṭikka; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: maṇṇappattam eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ ṇakara īṟu akkup peṟ ṟatu.
   maṇṇaṅkaṭṭi eṉa ammuppeṟṟatu. poṉṉappattam eṉa ṉakara īṟu akkup
   peṟṟatu. poṉṉaṅkaṭṭi eṉa ammup peṟṟatu. kāṉaṅ kōḻi eṉa vēṟṟumaikkaṇ
   ammuppeṟṟatu. maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi kāṉāṅ kōḻi eṉpaṉa marūu. vēyiṉṟalai eṉa
   yakara īṟṟu urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttuk
   keṭukka. nīrkuṟitu eṉa rakara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyiṟṟu. vērkuṟitu
   vērkkuṟitu itu rakara īṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci. vaṭacārkkūrai mēlcārkkūrai
   ivai valleḻuttu mikka marūu muṭipu. amparkkoṇṭāṉ impark koṇṭāṉ
   umparkkoṇṭāṉ emparkkoṇṭāṉ eṉa iv vīṟu ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉa
   valloṟṟuppeṟṟaṉa. takarkkuṭṭi pukarppōttu eṉpaṉa paṇputtokai karutiṟṟēl
   īṇṭu muṭikka. vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ muṉṉar muṭiyum. viḻaleṉṉum lakara īṟu
   vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ṟakaramākātu ṉakaramāy muṭital koḷka. viḻaṉkāṭu ceṟu tāḷ
   puṟam eṉa varum. kallampāṟai ucilaṅkōṭu eliyālaṅkāy puṭōlaṅkāy eṉa av
   vīṟu ammuppeṟṟatu kallāmpāṟai eṉpatu marūu. aḻalattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa av
   vīṟu attuppeṟṟatu. aḻukkaṟpōr puḻukkaṟcōṟu eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟu
   alvaḻittiripu. yāḻkuṟitu eṉpatu ḻakara īṟṟu alvaḻi yiyalpu. vīḻkuṟitu
   vīḻkkuṟitu eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟu alvaḻiyuṟaḻcci. tāḻappāvai eṉpatu av vīṟṟu
   alvaḻi akkuppeṟṟatu. yāḻiṉkōṭu ceykai talai puṟam eṉa av vīṟṟu
   urupiṟkuc ceṉṟacāriyai poruṭ kaṭ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻkka. muṉṉāḷai
   vāḻvu muṉṉāḷaipparicu orunāḷaikkuḻavi oru tiṅkaḷai*kkuḻavi eṉṟāṟ
   pōlvaṉa ḷakāra īṟu aikāramum ataṉōṭu valleḻuttum peṟutal koḷka. piṟavum
   ivvōttiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa vellāṅ koṇarntu itaṉāṉ muṭikka. kuḷattiṉ
   puṟam marattiṉpuṟam eṉa urupiṟku eytiya attōṭu iṉpeṟutaluṅ koḷka.
   iṉik "kaṭicollillai"(col-452) eṉpataṉāṉ vaḻakkiṉkaṇ ṇuñ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum
   vantu tirintu muṭiyuñ coṟkaḷum uḷa. avaṟṟaik kaṇṇiṉar koḷavē eṉpataṉāṉ
   maṇṇukkuppōṉāṉ poṉṉukkuviṟṟāṉ poruḷukkuppōṉāṉ nellukkuviṟṟāṉ koḷḷukkuk
   koṇṭāṉ patiṉēḻu eṉṟāṟpōla vaḻakkiṉkaṇ ukaram pepaṟuvaṉavum
   "viṇṇukkumēl" "maṇṇukku nāppaṇ"
   "pallukkut tōṟṟa paṉimullai paiṅkiḷikaḷ
   collukkut tōṟṟiṉṉun tōṟṟaṉavāl-nellukku
   nūṟōoo nūṟeṉpāḷ nuṭaṅkiṭaikkum vaṉmulaikku
   māṟōmā laṉṟaḷanta maṇ"
   eṉṟāṟpōlac ceyyuṭkaṇ ukarampeṟuvaṉavum, piṟavum muṭikka.
   paṟku neṟku eṉpaṉa mutaliyavuṅ koḷka. ivai urupiṉ poruḷ paṭa vārātu
   urupiṉkaṇ vantaṉavēṉum īṇṭuk kāṭṭiṉām. āṇṭap "puḷḷiyiṟutiyum" (eḻu-202)
   eṉṉum urupiyaṟcūttirattu ilēcu kōṭaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟeṉṟu karuti. iṉi
   accūttirattut "tēruṅkālai" eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭittalum oṉṟu. (110)
   puḷḷimayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
   ---------------------

                      9. kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyal
(kuṟṟiyalukara īṟukaṭkuriya puṇarcci yilakkaṇam uṇarttuvatu.)
                     9.1. kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ iyalpu

   kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ peyar, tiṟai, tokai
   406 . īreḻut torumoḻi uyirttoṭa riṭaittoṭar
   āytat toṭarmoḻi vaṉṟoṭar meṉṟoṭar
   āyiru mūṉṟē ukaraṅ kuṟukiṭaṉ.
   eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttuk kaṟṟiyalukara meṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa eḻuttup
   poruṭpeyarōṭum eṇṇuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟōṭum puṇarum muṟaimai
   uṇarttiṉamaiyiṟ kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. itu
   "meyyēyuyireṉṟāyīriyala"(eḻu-103) eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ uyiriṉatu vikāramāyniṉṟa
   kuṟṟukarattai irumoḻik kaṇṇum puṇarkkiṉṟamaiyiṉ mēlai ōttiṉōṭa
   iyaipuṭaittāyiṟṟu. ittalaiccūttiram moḻimarapiṉakattu iruvaḻiya eṉṟa
   kuṟṟukaram itaṉakattu iṉaittu moḻiyiṟuti varumeṉṟu avaṟṟiṟkup peyarum
   muṟaiyun tokaiyum uṇarttakiṉṟatu. appeyar peyar, ammuṟai muṟai, attokai
   tokai, "toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟu" (eḻu-36) varumeṉṟu āṇṭuk kūṟiyavataṉai īṇṭu
   aintuvakaippaṭutti ataṉōṭu "neṭṭeḻuttimparum" (eḻu-36) eṉṟatu oṉṟacē
   yātaliṉ ataṉaiyuṅ kūṭṭi aṟuvakaitteṉṟār,.
   i-ḷ: īreḻut torumoḻi -iraṇṭeḻut tāṉākiya orumoḻiyum, uyirt
   toṭar-uyirmēlvarum meyyait toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, iṭait toṭar-iṭai
   yoṟṟu mēlvarum meyyait toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āytat toṭarmoḻi
   -āytamākiya eḻuttu mēlvarum meyyait toṭarntu niṉṟa collum,
   vaṉṟoṭar-valloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyait toṭarntu niṉṟa collum,
   meṉṟoṭar-melloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyait toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āyiru
   mūṉṟē-ākiya avvāṟu collumē, ukaraṅ kuṟuku iṭaṉ-kuṟṟiyalukaraṅ
   kuṟukivarum iṭaṉ; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: nāku varaku teḷku eḵku kokku kuraṅku eṉa varum. itaṉai ēḻeṉṟu1
   koḷvārkkup piṇṇākku cuṇṇāmpu āmaṇakku mutaliyaṉa muṭiyāmai uṇarka. (1)
   ---------
   1 eḻuvakai iṭamāvaṉa:- 1 neṭiṟkīḻum, 2 neṭiloṟṟiṉ kīḻum 3 kuṟiliṇaik
       kīḻum 4 kuṟiviṇai yoṟṟiṉkīḻum 5 kuṟiṉeṭiṟ kīḻum 6 kuṟiṉeṭivoṟṟiṉ
       kīḻum 7 kuṟṟoṟṟiṉ kīḻu ma ṉa ivai; ivaṟṟukku eṭuttukkāṭṭu: muṟaiyē:
       1nāku 2 nākku 3 varaku 4 arakku 5 palācu 6 paṉāṭṭu 7 kācu eṉpaṉa.
       ivaṟṟukku nūṟpā:
       "neṭilē kuṟiliṇai kuṟiṉeṭi leṉṟivai
       yoṟṟoṭu varutaloṭu kuṟṟoṟ ṟiṟutiyeṉ
       ṟēḻkuṟ ṟukarak kiṭaṉeṉa moḻipa"
       eṉpatu.

   ---------
   avaṟṟuḷ, īroṟṟut toṭarkaḷ
   407. avaṟṟuḷ ,
   īroṟṟut toṭarmoḻi iṭaittoṭa rākā.
   iḵtu avvāṟaṉuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu.
   i-ḷ: avaṟṟuḷ-avvāṟaṉuḷ, īroṟṟut toṭar moḻi-iraṇṭoṟṟu iṭaikkaṇ toṭarnatu
   niṟkuñ colliṟku iṭaiyiṉa oṟṟu mutal niṉṟāl, iṭait toṭarākā-mēl
   iṭaiyiṉan toṭarntu nillā, valliṉamum melliṉamun toṭarntu niṟkum; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: ārkku īrkku noympu moympu eṉa varum. (2)
   -----------
   iruvaḻiyiṉum kuṟṟukaram varumāṟu
   408. allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
   ellā iṟutiyum ukara nilaiyum.*
   iḵtu "iṭaippaṭiṟkuṟuku miṭaṉumāruṇṭē" (eḻu-37) eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarmoḻikkaṇ
   araimāttiraiyiṉuṅ kuṟukumeṉa eytiyataṉai vilakki "avviyal nilaiyu mēṉai
   mūṉṟē" (eḻu-12) eṉṟa vitiyē peṟu meṉkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉum-alvaḻiyaic collumiṭattum, vēṟṟumaik
   kaṇṇum-vēṟṟumaip puṇarccik kaṇṇum, ellā iṟutiyum ukaram nilaiyum--āṟu
   īṟṟiṉ
   kaṇṇum ukaran taṉ arai māttiraiyaip peṟṟu niṟkum; e-ṟu
   varumoḻiyāṉallatu alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaiyum viḷaṅkāmaiyiṉ "allatu
   kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum" eṉavē irumoḻip puṇarcci eṉpatu peṟṟām.
   ivvirumoḻikkaṭ paḻaiya araimāttirai peṟṟē niṟkumeṉṟār. aṉṟi irumoḻip
   puṇarccikkaṇ oru māttirai peṟu meṉṟārkkup paṉmoḻip puṇarcciyākiya
   ceyyuḷilakkaṇaṅ kuṟṟukarattāṉ nērpacai niraipacai kōṭalum avaṟṟāṉ
   aṟupatu vañciccīrkōṭalum pattoṉpatāyirat tirunūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟoru toṭai
   kōṭalum iṉṟāy, muṟṟiyalukaramākavē koḷḷa vēṇṭutaliṉ māṟu koḷak
   kūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟan taṅkumeṉṟu uṇarka.
   e-ṭu: nākukaṭitu varakukaṭitu nākukaṭumai varakukaṭumai eṉa varum.
   ivaitam araimāttirai peṟṟaṉa. ēṉaiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
   -------
   * niṟaiyum eṉavum pāṭam. ippāṭattaiyē iḷampūraṇarum pērāciriyarum
       koṇṭaṉar, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyeḻuttuppōla araimāttirai yolip piṉum
       taṉittu niṟkumpōtu taṉittolikkum uyirttaṉmai nirampiyatē eṉpatu
       pērāciriyar karuttu.

   iṉi itu 'mālyāṟupōntu kālcurantupāyntu' eṉat toṭar moḻikkaṇṇum
   araimāttirai peṟṟatu eṉṉākkāl vañciccī riṉṟāmāṟu uṇarka. (3)
   ----------
   ataṟku, mēlum oru muṭipu
   409. valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻit
   tollai iyaṟkai nilaiyalu murittē.
   itu muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ araimāttirai peṟum eṉṟataṉai vilakki
   'iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟukumiṭaṉum' (eḻu - 37) eṉṟataṉāṉ arai māttiraiyiṉuṅ
   kuṟukumeṉṟu āṇṭu vitittatu īṇṭu valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇē
   varumeṉkiṟatu.
   i - ḷ : valloṟṟut toṭar moḻi -- valloṟṟut toṭar moḻik kuṟṟukaram,
   valleḻuttu varuvaḻi -- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu,
   tollai iyaṟkai nilaiyilum urittu -- 'iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟukum' (eḻu-37)
   eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiya arai māttiraiyiṉuṅ kuṟuki niṟkum eṉṟa iyalpilē
   niṟṟalum urittu; e - ṟu.
   ummai etirmaṟai.
   e - ṭu: kokkukkaṭitu kokkukkaṭumai eṉa araimāttiraiyiṟ kuṟaintavāṟu
   kuraṅakukaṭiteṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu paṭuttuc cevikaruviyāka uṇarka.
   muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattu* ukaraniṟaiyumeṉṟu pāṭam ōti ataṟku ukaram
   araimāttiraiyiṟ ciṟitu mikku niṟkumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi ic cūttirattiṟkup
   paḻaiya araimāttiraipeṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar. (4)
   -------------

                           9.2. kuṟṟiyalikaram

   kuṟṟiyalikaram varumāṟu
   410. yakaram varuvaḻi ikaraṅ kuṟukum
   ukarak kiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu.
   itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattu varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : yakaram varuvaḻi ukarakkiḷavi tuvarat tōṉṟātu-- yakara
   mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu nilaimoḻik kuṟṟukara veḻuttu muṟṟat
   tōṉṟātu, ikaraṅ kuṟukum-- āṇṭu ōr ikaram vantu arai māttiraipeṟṟu
   niṟkum; e - ṟu.
   e - ṭu : nākiyātu varakiyātu teḷkiyātu eḵkiyātu kokkiyātu kuraṅkiyātu
   eṉavarum. 'tuvara' eṉṟār, āṟu īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum ukaraṅ keṭumeṉṟaṟku (5)
   -----------
   * cīranilai kōṭaṟakaṇa ilavāciriyarum niṟaiyumeṉṟāḷupa.

   -----------

                    9.3. kuṟṟukarap potup puṇarcci

   neṭiṟṟoṭar, uyirttoṭar
   411. īreḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum
   vēṟṟumai yāyiṉ oṟṟiṭai iṉamikat
   tōṟṟam vēṇṭum valleḻuttu mikuti.
   itu muṟkūṟiya āṟaṉuḷ muṉṉarniṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci
   muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ṉ: īreḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum--- īreḻuttorumoḻik kuṟṟukara
   īṟṟiṟkum uyirttoṭar moḻik kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟkum, vēṟṟumaiyāyiṉ---vēṟṟumaip
   poruṭpuṇarcciyāyiṉ, iṉa oṟṟu iṭai mika---iṉamākiya oṟṟu iṭaiyilē mika,
   valleḻuttu mikuti tōṟṟam vēṇṭum---valleḻuttu mikuti tōṉṟi muṭitalai
   virumpum āciriyaṉ; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: yāṭṭukkāl cevi talai puṟam eṉavum, muyiṟṟukkāl ciṉaitalai puṟam
   eṉavum varum. kayiṟṟup puṟam vayiṟṟuttī eṉpaṉavumām.
   tōṟṟam eṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉaikkaṇattum immuṭipu koḷka. yāṭṭu ñāṟci niṇam maṇi
   vāl ataḷ eṉavum, muyiṟṟuñāṟci niṇam muṭṭaivalimai aṭai āṭṭam eṉavum
   varum. (6)
   ------------
   ataṟku, vēṟu muṭipu
   412. oṟṟiṭai iṉamikā moḻiyumā ruḷavē
   attiṟat tillai valleḻuttu mikalē.
   iḵtu eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: oṟṟu iṭai iṉam mikā moḻiyumāruḷa—muṟ kūṟiya iraṇṭaṉuḷ iṉavoṟṟu
   iṭaimikku muṭiyāta moḻi kaḷum uḷa, valleḻuttu mikal
   attiṟattillai—valloṟṟu mikkumuṭital akkūṟṟuḷillai; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: nākukāl cevi talai puṟam eṉavum, varaku katir ciṉai tāḷ
   patar eṉavum varum.
   attiṟameṉṟataṉāṉ urupiṟku eytiya cāriyai poruṭkaṭ ceṉṟa vaḻi iyaipu
   valleḻuttu vīḻkka. yāṭṭiṉkāl muyiṟṟiṉkāl nākiṉkāl varakiṅkatir eṉa
   varum.
   attiṟameṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉaikkaṇattum oṟṟiṭai mikāmai koḷka. nākuñāṟci nīṭci
   māṭci valimai eṉa oṭṭuka. (7)
   -----------
   iṭaittoṭar āytattoṭar
   413. iṭaiyoṟṟut toṭarum āytat toṭarum
   naṭaiā iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar.
   iḵtu iṭainiṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ: iṭaiyoṟṟut toṭarum āytattoṭarum - iṭaiyoṟṟut toṭarmoḻik
   kuṟṟukara īṟum āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, naṭai ā iyala eṉmaṉār
   pulavar - naṭaipeṟa naṭakkumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya avviyalpu muṭipiṉaiyuṭaiya
   eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; e - ṟu.
   e - ṭu: teḷkukāl ciṟai talai puṟam eṉavum, eḵkukaṭumai ciṟumai tīmai
   perumai eṉavum varum. (8)
   --------
   vaṉṟoṭar, meṉṟoṭar
   414. vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
   vanta valleḻut toṟṟiṭai mikumē
   melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi melloṟ ṟellām valloṟ ṟiṟuti kiḷaiyoṟ ṟākum.
   itu piṉṉiṉṟa iraṇṭiṟkum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ: vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum - vaṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum
   meṉṟoṭar moḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, vanta valleḻuttu oṟṟu iṭai mikum -
   varumoḻiyāy vanta valleḻuttiṉatu oṟṟu iṭaiyilē mikku muṭiyum, melloṟṟut
   toṭarmoḻi melloṟṟellām - avviraṇṭaṉuḷ melloṟṟut toṭarmoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa
   melloṟṟellām, iṟuti valloṟṟu - iṟutikkaṇiṉṟa valloṟṟum, kiḷai oṟṟu ākum
   - kiḷaiyākiya valloṟṟumāy muṭiyum; e - ṟu.
   iṟuti valloṟṟu varutalāvatu kuṟṟukaram ēṟiniṉṟa valloṟṟuttāṉē
   muṉṉarvantu niṟṟalām. kiḷaivalloṟṟu varutalāvatu ṇakārattiṟku ṭakāramum
   ṉakārattiṟku ṟakāramum puṇarcciyum piṟappum nōkkik kiḷaiyāmātaliṉ, avai
   muṉṉar vantu niṟṟalām.
   e - ṭu: kokkukkāl ciṟaku talai puṟam, kuraṅkukkāl cevi talai puṟam,
   kurakkukkāl cevi tali puṟam, eṭkukkuṭṭi cevi talai puṟam, eṟpukkāṭu
   curam, talai puṟam eṉa varum.1 aṟputtaḷai eṉpatu aṉpiṉāṟ ceyta
   taḷaiyeṉa vēṟṟumaiyum aṉpākiya taḷaiyeṉa alvaḻiyumām.
   vanta eṉṟataṉāṉ ivviraṇṭiṟkum urupiṟkuc ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṭ
   ceṉṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. kokkiṉkāl kuraṅkiṉkāl eṉa varum.
   ellāmeṉṟataṉāṟ paṟampiṟpāri kuṟumpiṟcāṉṟār eṉa melloṟṟut tiriyāmaiyuṅ
   koḷka.
   ----------
   1. kokkukkāl - vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram vanta valleḻuttu
       mikkumuṭintatu. kuraṅkukkāl - meṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiyalukaram vanta
       valleḻuttu mikkumuṭintatu. kurakkukkāl - meṉṟoṭariṉ iṭaiyil niṉṟa
       melloṟṟu iṟuti valloṟṟāyt tirintatu. eṭṭukkuṭṭi - meṉṟoṭariṉ
       iṭaiyil niṉṟa melloṟṟu iṉa valloṟṟāyt tirintatu.

   oṟṟeṉṟa mikutiyāṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum kurakkuñāṟci niṇam mukam viral
   ukir eṉa melloṟṟut tirintuvarumāṟu koḷka. cilappatikārameṉpatum atu.
   vaṉṟoṭarmoḻi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ varutal 'ñanamayava' (eḻu - 144)
   eṉapataṉāṉ muṭiyum. (9)
   ---------
   marappeyar
   415. marappeyark kiḷavik kammē cāriyai.
   iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, ammu vakuttaliṉ.
   i - ḷ: marappeyark kiḷavikku ammē cāriyai – kuṟṟiya lukara īṟṟu
   marappeyarkku varuñ cāriyai ammuccāriyai; e - ṟu.
   e - ṭu: tēkkaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.
   kamukaṅkāy teṅkaṅkāy cīḻkampul kampampul payaṟṟaṅkāy eṉṟāṟ pōlum
   pulliṉaiyum marameṉa aṭakki māṟukoḷak kūṟaleṉat taḻīik koṇṭa
   citaiveṉpatām ic cūttirameṉṟu uṇarka. (10)
   ---------------
   ataṟku, mēlum oru muṭipu
   416. melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyaru muḷavē.
   itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻikku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ: melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyarum uḷa -
   melloṟṟu valloṟṟākat tiriyātu melloṟṟāy muṭiyum
   marappeyarum uḷa; e - ṟu.
   e - ṭu: puṉkaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kuruntaṅkōṭu cetiḷ
   tōl pū eṉavum varum.
   valiyā marappeyarumuḷa eṉavē valikkum marappeyarum uḷaveṉṟu koḷka.
   vēppaṅkōṭu kaṭappaṅkāy īccaṅkulai eṉa varum. (11)
   -------
   neṭiṟṟoṭar, vaṉṟoṭar
   417. īreḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟut toṭarum
   ammiṭai varaṟku muriyavai uḷavē
   ammara poḻukum moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
   iḵtu īreḻut torumoḻikkum vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkum eytāta teytuvittatu, muṉṉar
   eytiyataṉai vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ.
   i - ḷ: īreḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟut toṭarum - īreḻuttorumoḻik
   kuṟṟiyalukaramum vaṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukaramum, am iṭai varaṟkum
   uriyavai uḷa - muṉ muṭittuppōnta muṭipukaḷaṉṟi ammuccāriyai iṭaiyēvantu
   muṭitaṟku uriyaṉavum uḷa; yāṇṭeṉiṉ, am marapu oḻukum.
   moḻivayiṉāṉa-avvilakkaṇam naṭakkum moḻiyiṭattu; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: ēṟaṅkōḷ cūtampōr vaṭṭampōr puṟṟampaḻañcōṟu eṉa varum,.
   ummai etirmaṟaiyātaliṉ, ammuppeṟātaṉa nākukāl kokkukkāl eṉa muṉṉark
   kāṭṭiṉavēyām.
   ammarapoḻukum eṉṟataṉāl aracakkaṉṉi muracak kaṭippu eṉa akkum
   valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttum aracavāḻkkai muracavāḻkkai eṉa akkukkoṭuttum
   muṭikka1.
   iṉṉum ataṉāṉē iruṭṭattukkoṇṭāṉ viḷakkattukkoṇṭāṉ eṉa attum valleḻuttuṅ
   koṭuttum mayilāppiṟkoṟṟaṉ paṟam piṟpāri eṉa iṉ koṭuttuṅ kariyataṉkōṭu
   neṭiyataṉkōṭu eṉa aṉkoṭuttum muṭikka. (12)
   ------------
   cila meṉṟoṭar moḻikaḷ
   418. oṟṟunilai tiriyā takkoṭu varūum
   akkiḷai moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.
   itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti
   vakukkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: oṟṟu nilai tiriyātu akkōṭu varūum-oṟṟu muṉṉiṉṟa nilaitiriyātu
   akkuccāriyaiyōṭum piṟa cāriyaiyōṭum varum, akkiḷai moḻiyum uḷa eṉa
   moḻipa-akkiḷaiyāṉa coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; e-ṟu
   itaṟku ummaiyai muṉṉar māṟuka.
   e-ṭu: kuṉṟakkūkai maṉṟappeṇṇai eṉa varum. ummaiyāṟ koṅkattuḻavu
   vaṅkattuvāṇikam eṉa attum peṟṟaṉa.
   nilaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟu nilaitiriyā atikārattukkaṇ varuñ cāriyaikku iyaipu
   valleḻuttu vīḻkka. akkiḷai yeṉṟār, iraṇṭu cāriyai toṭarntu muṭivaṉavum
   uḷaveṉṟaṟku. pārppaṉakkuḻavi cēri tōṭṭam piḷḷai eṉa aṉṉum akkum
   vantaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku uṭaimai virikka. pārppiṉuṭkuḻavi eṉṟumām.
   pārppāṉākiya kuḻavi eṉṟāl īṇṭu muṭiyāteṉṟu uṇarka1. pārppaṉamakaṉ
   pārppaṉavaṉitai eṉpaṉavum pārppāṉcāti uṇarttiṉa.
   --------
   1 aracakkaṉṉi aracavāḻkkai mutaliya toṭarkaḷiṉ iṭaiyil vantuḷḷatu
       akkuccāriyai eṉpataiviṭa akarac cāriyai eṉpatē poruttamām.

   ---------
   eṇṇuppeyar
   419. eṇṇuppeyark kiḷavi urupiyal nilaiyum
   itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyaroṭu poruṭ peyar muṭikkiṉṟatu.
   1 ivviyal kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyalātalāl, pārppu eṉṉuñ collaṉṟip
   pārppāṉ eṉṉuñcol iṅku nilaimoḻiyākātu eṉpatu karuttu.
   i-ḷ: eṇṇuppeyark kiḷavi-eṇṇuppeyarākiya coṟkaḷ poruṭpeyarōṭu
   puṇarumiṭattu, urupiyal nilaiyum-urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu
   aṉpeṟṟup puṇarum; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: oṉṟaṉkāyam iraṇṭaṉkāyam cukku tōrai payaṟu eṉa oṭṭuka.
   oṉṟaṉāṟkoṇṭaḷa kāyameṉa viriyum. varumoḻi varaiyātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ,
   iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇuṅ koḷka. oṉṟaṉ ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai eṉa
   varum. mēlaic cūttirattu "nilai" (eḻu- 418) eṉṟataṉāṉ iyaipu valleḻuttu
   vīḻkka. (14)
   -------------

                  9.4. kuṟṟukarac ciṟappup puṇarcci

   "vaṇṭu" "peṇṭu" eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ
   420. vaṇṭum peṇṭum iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
   itu meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟa muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: vaṇṭum peṇṭum iṉṉoṭu civaṇum-vaṇṭeṉṉuñ collum peṇṭeṉṉuñ collum
   iṉcāriyaiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: vaṇṭiṉkāl peṇṭiṉkāl eṉa varum. itaṟkumuṟkūṟiya ilēciṉāṉ
   valaleḻuttu vīḻkka. (15)
   --------
   "peṇṭu" eṉṉuñ collukku mēlum oru muṭipu.
   421. peṇṭeṉ kiḷavik kaṉṉum varaiyār,
   itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: peṇṭu eṉ kiḷavikku aṉṉum varaiyār -- peṇṭeṉṉuñ coṟku iṉṉēyaṉṟi
   aṉcāriyai varutalaiyum nīkkār āciriyar; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: peṇṭaṉkai cevi talai puṟam eṉa varum (16)
   -------------
   viṉāppeyar, cuṭṭuppeyar.
   422. yāteṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
   āyta iṟutiyum urupiyal nilaiyum.
   iḵtu īreḻuttorumoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattuḷ oṉṟaṟkuñ cuṭṭu mutalākiya āytat
   toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum vēṟu muṭipu
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: yātu eṉ iṟutiyuñ cuṭṭu mutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum-yāteṉṉum īṟuñ
   cuṭṭeḻuttu mutalākiya āytattoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum, urupiyal
   nilai
   yum-urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aṉpeṟ
   ṟuc cuṭṭumutaliṟuti āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: yātaṉkōṭu ataṉkōṭu itaṉkōṭu utaṉkōṭu cevitalai puṟam eṉa varum.
   āytaṅkeṭāmuṉṉē aṉṉiṉ akarattaik kuṟṟukarattiṉ mēl ēṟṟuka, āytaṅkeṭṭāl
   atu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ. (17)
   ---------------
   alvaḻiyiṟ cuṭṭup peyar
   423. muṉuyir varumiṭat tāytap puḷḷi
   maṉṉal vēṇṭum alvaḻi yāṉa.
   itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cuṭṭumutalukarattiṟku oruvaḻi alvaḻimuṭipa
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: muṉṉuyir varumiṭattu-cuṭṭumutalākiya āytat toṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara
   īṟṟiṉmuṉṉē uyirmutaṉ moḻi varumiṭattu, āytappuḷḷi maṉṉal
   vēṇṭum-āytavoṟṟu muṉpōlak keṭātu nilaipeṟṟu muṭitalai virumpum
   āciriyaṉ; alvaḻiyāṉa -alvaḻikkaṇ ; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉa niṟutti aṭai āṭai ilai īyam ural ūrti eḻu ēṇi
   aiyam oṭukkam ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.
   muṉṉeṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum iv viti koḷka. aḵ taṭaivu aḵtoṭṭam1 eṉa
   oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka. iṉṉumitaṉāṉē ēṉai ilakkaṇam
   muṭiyumāṟu aṟintu muṭikka. (18)
   ---------
   1 aḵtoṭṭam-ataṉai yoṭṭutal

   -------------
   alvaḻiyil ataṟku mēlum oru muṭipu.
   424. ēṉaimuṉ variṉē tāṉilai iṉṟē
   itu mēlavaṟṟiṟkup piṟakaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: ēṉai muṉ variṉ-muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉar uyirkkaṇamallaṉa
   varumāyiṉ, tāṉ nilaiyiṉṟu-avvāytaṅ keṭṭu muṭiyum; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: atukaṭitu itukaṭitu utukaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu
   māṇṭatu yātu valitu eṉa oṭṭuka. (19)
   ----------
   potuvāka alvaḻiyiṟ kuṟṟikara īṟukaḷ
   425. allatu kiḷappiṉ ellā moḻiyuñ
   colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟkai yākum.
   iḵtu āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟukarattiṟkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉ-alvaḻiyaic collumiṭattu, ellāmoḻiyum -āṟu īṟṟuk
   kuṟṟukaramum,colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟkaiyākum-mēl āciriyaṉ kūṟiya
   kuṇattaiyuṭaiya iyalpāy muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: nākukaṭitu varakukaṭitu teṉkukaṭitu eḵkukaṭitu kuraṅkukaṭitu
   ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. ēṉaik kaṇattukkaṇ "niṉṟacoṉmuṉiyalpākum"
   (eḻu-144) eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka.
   ellāmoḻiyum eṉṟataṉāl viṉaiccollum viṉaikkuṟippuc collum iyalpāymuṭital
   koḷka. kiṭantatukutirai karitukutirai eṉa varum.
   colliya eṉṟataṉāṉ irupeyaroṭṭuppaṇputtokai vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟumikku
   valleḻuttuppeṟṟu muṭitalum iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟaṟumikku muṭitaluṅ
   koḷka. karaṭṭukkāṇam kuruṭṭuk kōḻi tiruṭṭup pulaiyaṉ kaḷiṟṟuppaṉṟi
   veḷiṟṟuppaṉai eyiṟṟuppal eṉavum, varaṭṭāṭu kuruṭṭerutu eṉavum varum.
   paṇpiṉeṉṟataṉāl melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu
   valloṟṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu
   valloṟṟākātu aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟumuṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka.
   ōryāṭṭaiyāṉai aiyāṭṭaiyerutu eṉavum, aṟṟaikkūttar iṟṟaikkūttar eṉavum,
   maṉṟaittūtai maṉṟaippāṉai paṇṭaiccāṉṟōr eṉavum varum. (20)
   ----------------
   vaṉṟoṭar
   426. valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikumē.
   iḵtu avvāṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituvitu vakuttatu,
   i-ḷ: valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikum -valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻik
   kuṟṟukara īṟu valleḻuttu varuvaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kokkukkaṭitu pākkukkaṭitu paṭṭukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa
   varum. (21)
   "āṅku" "yāṅku" mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻikaḷ
   -------------
   427. cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
   yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṭoṭar moḻiyum
   āyiyal tiriyā valleḻut tiyaṟkai.
   itavum av āṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉkaṇ ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa
   iṭaiccoṟku muṭipukūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: cuṭṭuc ciṉai nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum- cuṭṭākiya ciṉaiyeḻuttu nīṇṭa
   meṉṟoṭark kuṟṟukara īṟum, yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum-yāveṉṉum
   viṉā mutalākiya meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟukara īṟum, valleḻuttiyaṟkai ā iyal
   tiriyā-valleḻuttuppeṟṟu muṭiyuntaṉmaiyākiya av viyalpiṟ ṟiriyātu
   muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: āṅkukkoṇṭāṉ īṅkukkoṇṭāṉ ūṅkukkoṇṭāṉ yāṅkuk koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ
   pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
   iyaṟkaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ meṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam
   iyalpāyum vaṉṟoṭarmoḻik kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉaiyeccam mikkum muṭivaṉa
   koḷka. iruntu koṇṭāṉ āṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ tantu tīrntāṉ vantu pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum,
   cettukkiṭantāṉ ceṟṟucceytāṉ uyttukkoṇṭāṉ naṭṭuppōṉāṉ eṉavum varum. (22)
   -------------
   yāṅku eṉpataṟku mēlum oru muṭipu.
   428. yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpu mākum.
   itu mēlaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   vallaḻuttuvilakki iyalpā meṉṟaliṉ.
   i-ḷ: yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpumākum-avaṟṟuḷ yāveṉṉum viṉāvaiyuṭaiya col
   muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi iyalpāyum muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: yāṅkukoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. iḵtu eppaṭiyeṉṉum
   viṉāpporuḷai uṇarttiṟṟu. uṇmaiyāṉ mikkumuṭitalē valiyuṭaittu. ēkāram
   pirinilai. (23)
   --------
   avaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻic ceykai
   429. annāṉ moḻiyan tannilai tiriyā
   itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻic ceykai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: annāṉmoḻiyum-cuṭṭu mutaṉ mūṉṟum yāmu taṉ moḻiyumākiya annāṉku
   moḻiyum, tam nilai tiriyā- tam melloṟṟāya taṉmai tirintu valloṟṟākātu
   muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: muṟkāṭṭiyavē. tannilai yeṉṟataṉāṉ mekalloṟṟut tiriyātu valleḻuttu
   mikkumuṭivaṉa piṟavuṅ koḷka. aṅkukkoṇṭāṉ iṅkukkoṇṭāṉ uṅkukkoṇṭāṉ
   eṅkukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.
   iṉi muṉṉar yā moḻiyeṉṉātu viṉāveṉṟataṉāṉ ēḻāvataṉ iṭapporuṭṭākiya
   piṟavum iyalpāy muṭivaṉavuṅ koḷka. muntu koṇṭāṉ paṇṭukoṇṭāṉ iṉṟukoṇṭāṉ
   aṉṟukoṇṭāṉ eṉṟukoṇṭāṉ eṉa varum. (24)
   ----------
   "uṇṭu" eṉṉuñ col
   430. uṇṭeṉ kiḷavi uṇmai ceppiṉ
   muntai iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum
   mēṉilai yoṟṟē ḷakāra mātalum
   āmuṟai yiraṇṭum urimaiyu muṭaittē
   valleḻuttu varūuṅ kālai yāṉa.
   itu meṉṟoṭar moḻiyuḷ viṉaikkuṟippāy niṉṟatōr coṟpaṇpai uṇarttuṅkāl
   vēṟumuṭipu peṟutal kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uṇṭeṉkiḷavi uṇmai ceppiṉ-uṇṭeṉṉuñ col viṉaikkuṟippai yuṇarttātu
   oru poruḷ tōṉṟuṅkāl tōṉṟi atu keṭuntuṇaiyum uṇṭāyniṟkiṉṟa taṉmaiyākiya
   paṇpai uṇarttiniṟkumāyiṉ, muntai iṟuti meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum-muṉṉar niṉṟa
   kuṟṟukaramtāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyoṭuṅ keṭutalum, mēṉilai oṟṟē ḷakāramātalum-
   ataṟku mēl niṉṟa ṇakāra oṟṟu ḷakāra oṟṟātalumākiya, ā muṟai iraṇṭum
   urimaiyum uṭaittu-ammuṟai yiṉaiyuṭaiya iraṇṭu nilaiyum urittu aḵtu
   urittaṉṟi muṉṉar niṉṟa nilaiyilē kēṭun tiriyum iṉṟi niṟṟalum uṭaittu,
   valleḻuttu varūuṅ kālai yāṉa-valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu;
   e-ṟu.
   valleḻuttu atikārattāl vārāniṟpa valleḻuttu varūuṅkālai eṉṟataṉāṉ
   avvirumuṭipum uḷatu paṇpai yuṇarttum pakaram varum moḻikkaṇṇē yeṉpatūum
   ēṉaik kacataṅkaḷilum iyalpukaṇattiṉum uṇṭeṉa niṉṟu viṉaikkuṟippāyuñ
   ciṟupāṉmai paṇpāyum niṟku meṉpatūuṅ koḷka.
   e-ṭu: uḷporuḷ uṇṭuporuḷ eṉa varum. itu poruṇmai cuṭṭātu uṇmait taṉmaip
   paṇpai īṇṭu uṇarttiṟṟu. iṉi uṇṭu kāṇam uṇṭucākkāṭu uṇṭutāmarai
   uṇṭuñāṉam nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭai āṭai eṉa varuvaṉavellāṅ kēṭun
   tiripumiṉṟi viṉaikkuṟippāyuñ ciṟupāṉmai paṇpāyum niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ
   vēṟupāṭu viṉaiyiyaluḷ viṉaikkuṟippu ōtum vaḻi uṇarka.
   uḷporuḷeṉpatu paṇputtokai muṭipaṉṟō veṉiṉ, aḵtu ōcai oṟṟumaipaṭac
   collum vaḻiyatu pōlum. iḵtu ōcai iṭaiyaṟavupaṭac collum vaḻiya teṉka.
   (25)
   ----------
   ticaippeyar
   431. iruticai puṇariṉ ēyiṭai varumē.
   itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟut ticaippeyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: iru ticai puṇariṉ-iraṇṭu perunticaiyun tammiṟ puṇariṉ, ē iṭai
   varum-ēyeṉṉuñ cāriyai iṭai niṉṟu puṇarum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: teṟkēvaṭakku kiḻakkēmēṟku ivai ummait tokai. (26)
   -------------
   ticaippeyar muṉ ticaippeyar
   432. tiripuvēṟu kiḷappiṉ oṟṟu mukaramuṅ
   keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar
   oṟṟumey tirintu ṉakāra mākum
   teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅ kālai yāṉa.
   itu perunticaikaḷōṭu kōṇatticaikaḷ puṇartataliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.
   i-ḷ: tiripuvēṟu kiḷappiṉ-apperun ticaikaḷōṭu kōṇat ticaikaḷai vēṟākap
   puṇarkku miṭattu, oṟṟum ukaramuṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār
   pulavar-avvukaram ēṟi niṉṟa oṟṟum av vīṟṟu ukaramuṅ keṭṭumuṭital
   vēṇṭumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar. teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅ kālai -atu teṟkeṉṉan
   ticaiyoṭu puṇaruṅ kālattu, āṉa oṟṟu mey tirintu ṉakāramākum-atticaikkup
   poruntiniṉṟa ṟakāra oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu tirintu ṉakara oṟṟāy niṟkum; e-ṟu.
   tirinteṉṟataṉāṉ vaṭakkeṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa kakara oṟṟukkeṭuttu muṭittuk
   koḷka.
   e-ṭu: vaṭakiḻakku vaṭamēṟku teṉkiḻakku teṉmēṟku eṉavarum.
   vēṟeṉṟataṉāl ticaippeyarōṭu poruṭpeyar variṉum immuṭipu koḷka.
   vaṭakaṭal vaṭacuram vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari teṉcuram teṉṉilaṅkai eṉa
   varum.
   meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ uyir keṭṭun tirintum mey keṭṭum muṭivaṉavum uḷa.
   ticaippeyarmuṉṉarp poruṭpeyar vantuḻi yeṉṟu uṇarka. kiḻakku eṉpatu
   karai kūrai eṉpavaṟṟōṭu puṇarumiṭattuk kīḻkarai kīḻkūrai eṉa
   nilaimoḻiyiṟuti ukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu ataṉmēlniṉṟa kakara oṟṟum
   ḻakarattil akaramuṅ keṭṭu mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭintaṉa. mēṟku, karai
   kūrai, mīkarai mīkūrai eṉa nilaimoḻi īṟṟu ukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu
   ataṉmēlniṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟuṅ keṭṭu ēkāram īkāramāki muṭintaṉa. iṉṉum
   itaṉāṉē mēṉmāṭu mēlpāl mēlaiccēri eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavuñ ceykaiyaṟintu
   muṭikka. (27)
   --------

                   9.5. kuṟṟukara eṇṇup puṇarcci

   pattoṭu eṇṇuppeyar
   433. oṉṟumuta lāka eṭṭa ṉiṟuti
   ellā veṇṇum pattaṉ muṉvariṟ
   kuṟṟiya lukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭumē
   muṟṟaiṉ varūum iraṇṭalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
   niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē āṟu īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaramum puṇarumāṟu uṇartti iṉi av vīṟṟu
   eṇṇuppeyar muṭikkiṉṟār: iḵtu avaṟṟuṭ patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarōṭu
   eṇṇuppeyarvantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭaṉ iṟuti ellā eṇṇum- oṉṟeṉṉum eṇ mutalāka
   eṭṭeṉṉum eṇṇīṟāyuḷḷa ellā eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, pattaṉ muṉ variṉ-patteṉṉum
   eṇṇup peyariṉ muṉ variṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭum-appatteṉṉuñ
   colliṟ kuṟṟukaran tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyōṭuṅ keṭum, iraṇṭalaṅkaṭai muṟṟa iṉ
   varūum-āṇṭu iraṇṭallāta eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṭattu muṭiya iṉcāriyai iṭaivantu
   puṇarum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: patiṉoṉṟu patiṉmūṉṟu patiṉāṉku patiṉāṟu patiṉēḻu patiṉeṭṭu eṉa
   varum.
   nilaimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṟ piṟaveṇṇiṉ muṉṉarp piṟapeyar vantuḻiyuma,
   iṉpeṟutal koḷka. oṉpatiṉkūṟu oṉpatiṉpāl eṉa varum. muṟṟaveṉṟataṉāṟ
   patiṉāṉkeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram varumoḻikkaṭ karuviceytu keṭuttu
   muṭikka. (28)
   ------------
   pattiṉ muṉ iraṇṭu
   434. pattaṉoṟ ṟukkeṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal
   otta teṉpa iraṇṭuvaru kālai.
   itu mēl iṉpeṟāteṉṟu vilakkiya iraṇṭaṟkup piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: pattaṉ oṟṟuk keṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal-patteṉṉuñ colliṉ niṉṟa takara
   oṟṟukkeṭa ṉakara oṟṟu iraṭṭittu varutal, iraṇṭu varukālai ottateṉpa-
   iraṇṭeṉṉumeṇ varuṅkālattiṟ poruntiṟ ṟeṉpar āciriyar: e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉa varum.
   "kuṟṟiyalukara meyyoṭuṅkeṭum" (eḻu-433) eṉṟa viti itaṟkum
   mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟkuṅ koḷka. (29)
   -----------
   pattiṉ muṉ āyiram
   435. āyiram variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu.
   iḵtu āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarvariṉ varum muṭipu kūṟu kiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: āyiram variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu-muṟkūṟiya pattaṉ muṉṉar oṉṟu
   mutaliyaṉavēyaṉṟi āyirameṉṉumeṇ vantālum īṟukeṭṭu iṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum
   iyalpil tiriyātu; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: patiṉāyiram eṉa varum.
   ummai iṟantatu taḻīiyaṟṟu. (30)
   -----------
   pattiṉmuṉ niṟai aḷavup peyarkaḷ
   436. niṟaiyu maḷavum varūuṅ kālaiyuṅ
   kuṟaiyā tākum iṉṉeṉ cāriyai.
   iḵtu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu niṟaippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum puṇarkiṉṟa puṇarcci
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: niṟaiyum aḷavum varūuṅ kālaiyum-muṟkūṟiya patteṉpataṉ muṉṉar
   niṟaippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varuṅkālattum, iṉṉeṉ cāriyai
   kuṟaiyātākum-avviṉṉeṉṉuñ cāriyai kuṟaiyātu vantu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: patiṉkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum, patiṉkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi
   maṇṭai vaṭṭi eṉavum, patiṟṟakal patiṟṟuḻakku eṉavum varum.
   kuṟaiyātāku meṉṟataṉāṟ poruṭpeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum
   vantuḻiyum iṉ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñceykai ceytu muṭikka. patiṟṟu1 vēli yāṇṭu
   aṭukku muḻam eṉavum, patiṉṟiṅkaḷ eṉavum, patiṟṟuttoṭi eṉavum varum.
   patiṟṟoṉṟu eṉpatupōla iraṇṭumutaṟ pattaḷavum oṭṭuka.
   iv vīṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal 'aḷavākumoḻimutal' (eḻu-121) eṉpataṉuḷ
   'nilaiiya' eṉṟataṉāl muṭikka. ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutalum ukaramvarutalum
   valleḻuttuppeṟutalum 'oṉṟumutalāka' (eḻu-433) eṉpataṉuḷ 'muṟṟa'
   eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. (31)
   -----------
   1 patiṟṟu eṉpatil iṟṟu cāriyai eṉpar pavaṇantiyār.
       2. oṉpāṉ eṉpatu oṉpatu eṉpataṉ maṟuvaṭivam. iḵtu iṉṟu kuṟikkum 9
       eṉṉum eṇṇukku mutalāvatu vaḻaṅkiṉa peyar koṇṭu eṉpatu.

   ----------
   eṇṇup peyaroṭu pattu
   437. oṉṟumuta loṉpāṉ2 iṟuti muṉṉar
   niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa āytam
   vantiṭai nilaiyum iyaṟkait teṉpa
   kūṟiya iyaṟkai kuṟṟiya lukaram
   āṟa ṉiṟuti alvaḻi yāṉa.
   iḵtu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu patteṉṉum eṇṇup peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ iṟutimuṉṉar-oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu īṟākak kūṟukiṉṟa
   eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, niṉṟa pattaṉ oṟṟukkeṭa-varumoḻiyāka vantu
   niṉṟa patteṉṉuñ colliṉatu takara oṟṟuk keṭa, āytam vantu iṭai nilaiyum
   iyaṟkait teṉpa-āytamāṉatu vantu iṭaiyē nilaipeṟum iyalpai yuṭaitteṉṟu
   kūṟuvar āciriyara, āṟaṉ iṟuti alvaḻiyāṉa-avaṟṟuḷ āṟeṉṉum īṟallāta
   viṭattu, kuṟṟiyalukaraṅ kūṟiya iyaṟkai-kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟkūṟiya
   iyaṟkaiyāy meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   iṅṅaṉam varumāṟu mēṟcūttiraṅkaḷuṭ kāṭṭutum. vanteṉṟataṉāl āytamākat
   tiriyātu takara oṟṟukkeṭṭu orupatu eṉṟu niṟṟaluṅkoḷka. (32)
   -----------
   ataṉkaṇ, ’oṉṟu’ iraṇṭu eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ
   438. mutalī reṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakara mākum
   ukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.
   itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku nilaimoḻicceykai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: mutalī reṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaram ākum-avaṟṟiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa
   iraṇṭeṇṇiṉuṭaiya ṉakara oṟṟum ṇakara oṟṟum rakara oṟṟākat tirintu
   niṟkum, āvayiṉāṉa ukaram varutal-avviṭattu ukaram varuka; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: orupaḵtu eṉa varum. oṉṟeṉpataṉ īṟṟuk kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu
   ṉakara oṟṟiṉai rakara oṟṟākki ukara mēṟṟi oruveṉa niṟutti niṉṟa
   patteṉpataṉ takara oṟṟuk keṭuttu āytamākkip paḵteṉa varuvittu orupaḵtu
   eṉa muṭikka. mēl varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkuñ cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kūṟuñ ciṟappuviti
   oḻintavaṟṟiṟku ituvē muṭipākak koḷka. (33)
   ------------
   ’iraṇṭu’ eṉpataṟkum mēlum oru muṭipu
   439. iṭainilai rakaram iraṇṭeṉ eṇṇiṟku
   naṭaimaruṅ kiṉṟē poruḷvayi ṉāṉa .
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: iraṇṭe ṉeṇṇiṟku iṭainilai rakaram- iraṇṭeṉṉu meṇṇiṟku iṭainiṉṟa
   rakaram, poruḷvayiṉāṉa- ammoḻi poruḷ peṟum iṭattu, naṭaimaruṅkiṉṟu
   -naṭakkum iṭamiṉṟik keṭum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: irupaḵtu eṉa varum. itaṟku rakaravuyirmey itaṉāṟ keṭuttu ēṉaiya
   kūṟiyavāṟē kūṭṭimuṭikka.
   poruḷeṉavē eṇṇallāp peyaruṅ koḷka. irukaṭal iruviṉai irupiṟappu eṉa
   varum. (34)
   ---------------
   ’mūṉṟu’ ’āṟu’
   440. mūṉṟu māṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum.
   ituvumatu.
   i-ḷ: mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum-mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇum āṟeṉṉu meṇṇum
   neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   aṟu eṉak kuṟukkip paḵtu eṉa varuvittu aṟupaḵtu eṉa muṭikka. (35)
   ------------
   mūṉṟu eṉpataṟku mēlum oru muṭipu.
   441. mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē pakāra mākum.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟē pakāramākum-mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟup
   pakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: muppaḵtu eṉa varum. (36)
   ---------
   "nāṉku" eṉṉuñ col
   442. nāṉka ṉoṟṟē ṟakāra mākum.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: nāṉkaṉ oṟṟē ṟakāramākum-nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu
   ṟakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: nāṟpaḵtu eṉa varum. (37)
   ----------
   "aintu" eṉṉuñ col
   443. ainta ṉoṟṟē makāra mākum.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē makāramākum-ainteṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu makara
   oṟṟāy muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: aimpaḵtu eṉa varum.
   ēḻu kuṟṟiyalukara īṟaṉmai urupiyaluṭ kāṇka. (38)
   ---------
   "eṭṭu" eṉṉuñ col
   444. eṭṭa ṉoṟṟē ṇakāra mākum.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: eṭṭaṉoṟṟē ṇakāramākum-eṭṭeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṭakara oṟṟu ṇakara
   oṟṟāy muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum. (39)
   ------------
   "oṉpatu" eṉṉuñ col
   445. oṉpāṉ okaramicait takaram oṟṟum
   muntai yoṟṟē ṇakāram iraṭṭum
   paḵteṉ kiḷavi āytapaka raṅkeṭa
   niṟṟal vēṇṭum ūkārak kiḷavi
   oṟṟiya takaram ṟakara mākum.
   iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu,.
   i-ḷ: oṉpāṉ okaramicait takaram oṟṟum- oṉpatu eṉa niṟuttip paḵtu eṉa
   varuvittu muṭikkuṅkāl, nilaimoḻiyākiya oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉatu okarattiṟku
   muṉṉāka oru takara oṟṟut tōṉṟi niṟkum. muntai oṟṟē ṇakāram iraṭṭum-muṉ
   coṉṉa ōkārattiṟku muṉṉar niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ṇakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu
   niṟkum, paḵteṉ kiḷavi āyta pakaraṅkeṭa-varumoḻiyākiya paḵteṉṉuñ col
   taṉkaṇ āytamum pakaramuṅ keṭṭuppōka, ūkārak kiḷavi niṟṟal
   vēṇṭum-nilaimoḻiyiyal iraṭṭiya ṇakarattiṉ piṉṉar ūkāramākiya
   eḻuttuvantu niṟṟalai āciriyaṉ virumpum,oṟṟiya takaram
   ṟakaramākum-varumoḻiyākiya patteṉpataṉ īṟṟiṉmēlēṟiya ukaraṅ keṭātu
   pirintuniṟpa oṟṟāy niṉṟa takaram ṟakara oṟṟāy niṟkum; e-ṟu.
   -------------
   1 innūṟpā oṉpatu+paḵtu = toṇṇūṟu eṉa muṭikkiṉṟatu. immuṭipu
       evvakaiyilum poruntāta toṉṟām. oṉpatu eṉṉum eṇṇukkup paḻampeyar
       toṇṭu eṉpatu. "toṇṭu talaiyiṭṭa" (tol. 1358) eṉṟu āciriyarum
       "toṇṭupaṭu tivavu" (malaipaṭu, 21) eṉṟu peruṅkuṉṟūrp peruṅ
       kaucikaṉārum kūṟutal kāṇka. toṇṭu eṉṉuñ col tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē
       vaḻakkaṟṟup pōyviṭṭatu. avar kālattiṟku muṉ, toṇṭu toṇpatu toṇṇūṟu
       toḷḷāyiram eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē 9, 90, 900, 9000 eṉṉum eṇkaḷaik kuṟikkum
       peyarkaḷāyiruntaṉa. "toṇṭu" eṉṉum oṉṟāmiṭappeyar vaḻakkaṟavē,
       toṇpatu eṉṉum pattā miṭappeyar oṉṟāmiṭattiṟkum, toṇṇūṟu eṉṉum nūṟā
       miṭappeyar pattā miṭattiṟkum, toḷḷāyiram eṉṉum āyirattā miṭappeyar
       nūṟā miṭattiṟkumāka vaḻaṅkat talaippaṭṭaṉa.

   toṇpatu eṉṉum peyar muṟaiyē toṉpatu oṉpatu eṉa maruviṟṟu. āyirattā
   miṭappeyar nūṟāmiṭattiṟku vaḻaṅkavē, 9000 eṉṉum eṇṇaik kuṟikka oṉpatu
   eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ āyiram eṉṉum peyaraic cērkka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. mutaṟ
   pattu eṇṇup peyarkaḷil oṉpatu eṉpatait tavira maṟṟavaiyellām oru collā
   yiruppataiyum, oṉpatu eṉpatu iru collāyp patu(pattu) eṉṟu muṭivataiyum
   toṇṇūṟu eṉpatu nūṟu eṉṟum toḷḷāyiram eṉpatu āyiram eṉṟum muṭivataiyum
   nōkkuka. toṇpatu eṉpatiṉ tiripāṉa oṉpatu eṉṉum collukkup poruntap
   pukalum muṟaipaṟṟi, oṉṟu kuṟainta pattu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvā cilar. atuvē
   ataṉ poruḷāyiṉ toṇṇūṟu toḷḷāyiram eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum apporuḷ ēṟkavēṇṭum.
   aṅṅaṉam ēlāmaiyiṉ atu pōliyuraiyeṉa maṟukka, ākavē,
   toṇṭu+pattu=toṇpatu, toṇṭu+nūṟu =toṇṇūṟu, toṇṭu+āyiram=toḷḷāyiram eṉṟu
   puṇarppatē muṟaiyeṉṟum, toṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar vaḻakkaṟṟatiṉāl ataṉ
   mēliṭap peyarkaḷ mūṉṟum ovvōriṭamāyt tāḻntuvantu vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟum
   aṟintukoḷka.
   e-ṭu: toṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. itaṉai oṟṟāy vantuniṉṟa takara oṟṟiṉmel
   nilaimoḻi okarattai ēṟṟit tovvākki ṇakara oṟṟu iraṭṭi ataṉmēl
   varumoḻikkaṭ pakaramum āytamuṅkeṭa vanta ūkāra mēṟṟit toṇṇūvākkip
   pakaravāytameṉṉāta muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ nilaimoḻikkaṭ pakaramum āytamuṅ
   keṭuttuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu varumoḻi iṟutit takara oṟṟut
   tirintuniṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉmēlē niṉṟa ukaramēṟṟit toṇṇūṟeṉa muṭikka. (40)
   -----------
   avveṇṇup peyarkaḷ muṉ aḷavu niṟaippeyarkaḷ
   446. aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum niṟaiyiṉ kiḷaviyuṅ
   kiḷanta iyala tōṉṟuṅ kālai.
   itu mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉeṇkaḷōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum
   muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum niṟaiyiṉ kiḷaviyun tōṉṟuṅ kālai-muṟkūṟiya
   oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷantaṟiyappaṭum aḷavuppeyarum
   niṟaippeyarum vantu tōṉṟuṅkālattu, kiḷanta iyala-āṟaṉ īṟu alvaḻik
   kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu mutalīreṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakaramāy ukaram vantu
   iṭainilai rakaraṅkeṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭu mutal kuṟuki nāṉkaṉoṟṟu
   vaṉkaṇattu ṟakaramāy eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakaramāy muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: orukalam irukalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭaivaṭṭi eṉavum,
   orukaḻañcu irukaḻañcu kaḵcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa
   muṉṉar muṭittum. ivai mutalīreṇṇiṉ ceykai.
   tōṉṟuṅkālaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ ivveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum
   muṭiyātuniṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷellām ivvitiyum piṟavitiyum eytuvittu
   muṭittukkoḷka. orumūṉṟu orunāṉku irumūṉṟu irunāṉku orukāl irukāl
   orumuntirikai irumuntirikai orumukkāl irumukkāl eṉpaṉa piṟavuṅ koṇarntu
   oṭṭuka. iṉip piṟaviti eytuvaṉa ōroṉṟu ōriraṇṭu ōraintu ōrāṟu ōrēḻu
   ōreṭṭu ōroṉpatu eṉavum, īroṉṟu īriraṇṭu īraintu īrēḻu īreṭṭu īroṉpatu
   eṉavum, mūvoṉṟu mūviraṇṭu mūvaintu mūvāṟu mūvēḻu mūveṭṭu mūvoṉpatu
   eṉavum, "mutalīreṇṇiṉ muṉuyir" (eḻu-455) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ uyirkku
   eytiya piṟavitiyum "mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉilai nīṭalumurittu" (eḻu-457) eṉṟa
   piṟavitiyum peṟṟup piṟaceykaikaḷum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa. nāloṉṟu nāliraṇṭu
   nālaintu nālāṟu nālēḻu nāleṭṭu nāloṉpatu eṉpaṉa "nāṉkaṉoṟṟē
   lakāramākum" (eḻu-453) eṉṟa viti peṟṟu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum ivvāṟēyaṉṟi
   aḷavum niṟaiyumaṇaṉṟi varuvaṉavellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka. (41)
   ---------
   ataṉkaṇ "mūṉṟu" eṉṉuñ col
   447. mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum.
   itu mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vantatokkum-mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu varumoḻiyāy
   vanta aḷavuppeyar niṟaippeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta valloṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāyt
   tirintu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: mukkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum, mukkaḻañcu kaḵcu toṭi palam
   eṉavum varum,.
   "nāṉkaṉoṟṟē ṟakāramākum" (eḻu-442) eṉṟa muṉṉai māṭṭēṟu niṟṟaliṉ
   nāṟkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum, nāṟkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum.
   (42)
   ---------
   "aintu" eṉṉuñcol
   448. ainta ṉoṟṟē melleḻut tākum.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē melleḻuttākum-aintāvataṉ kaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu varumoḻi
   valleḻuttukku ēṟṟa melleḻuttākat tirintu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: aiṅkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai eṉavum, aiṅkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum
   varum. ēkāram īṟṟacai. (43)
   ------------
   avviraṇṭiṉ mēṉ muṭipukaṭku varaiyaṟai
   449. kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūuṅ kālai.
   itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṟkum aintaṟkum varumoḻi varaiyaṟukkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūuṅ kālai-mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu vantatoppatūum
   aintaṉoṟṟu melleḻuttāvatūum avvaḷavuppeyar oṉpatiṉum vaṉkaṇamākiya
   kacatapakkaḷ mutaṉ moḻiyāy vanta iṭattu; e-ṟu.
   atu muṉṉark kāṭṭiṉām. āṟu "neṭumutal kuṟukum" (eḻu-440) eṉṟa māṭṭēṟṟāṉē
   āṟu neṭumutal kuṟukiniṉṟatu.
   e-ṭu: aṟukalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi eṉavum, aṟukaḻañcu
   toṭi palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa mēṟkāṭṭiṉām. ēḻu kuṟṟukara
   īṟaṉmaiyiṉ māṭṭēṟu ēlātāyiṟṟu. (44)
   ---------
   "eṭṭu" eṉṉuñ col
   450. namava eṉṉum mūṉṟōṭu civaṇi
   yakaram variṉum eṭṭaṉmu ṉiyalpē.
   itu vēṇṭātukūṟi 1vēṇṭiyatu muṭikkiṉṟatu. 2"ñanamayava" (eḻu-144) eṉṉuñ
   cūttirattuṭ kuṟiyavaṟṟaik kūṟutaliṉ.
   i-ḷ: eṭṭaṉmuṉ-eṭṭeṉpataṉ muṉṉar. namava eṉṉum mūṉṟōṭu civaṇi akaram
   variṉum-aḷavuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar meṉkaṇattu iraṇṭum iṭaikkaṇattu
   oṉṟumākiya namava eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉōṭu porunti uyirkkaṇattu akaram variṉum,
   ummaiyāṉ uyirkkaṇattu ukaram variṉuṅ kūṟāta valleḻuttukkaḷ variṉum,
   iyalpu-muṟkūṟiyavāṟē ṭakāram ṇakāramāy vēṟōr vikāramiṉṟi iyalpāy
   muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   namavaveṉṉum mūṉṟum vantāṟpōla akaramvariṉu meṉpatu poruḷ.
   e-ṭu: eṇṇāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi eṉavum eṇṇakal eṇṇuḻakku eṉavum, eṇkalam cāṭi
   tūtai pāṉai eṉavum varum.
   oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ vaṉkaṇattu niṟaippeyaruṅ koḷka. eṇkaḻañcu toṭi palam
   eṉa varum.
   iv vēṇṭā kūṟalāṉ eṇṇakaleṉak kuṟṟukara īṟāyk kēṭun tirivum peṟṟu
   uyirvarumoḻiyāṉa toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. (45)
   -----------
   1 vēṇṭātu kūṟal-ñanamavaya (eḻu-144) eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiyataik kūṟal
       2 vēṇṭiyatu muṭittal-uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu nilaimoḻiyīṟṟu mey
       iraṭṭutalaik koḷḷutal.

   ---------
   "aintu" "mūṉṟu" eṉṉum iraṇṭu coṟkaḷukkum otta muṭipu
   451. aintum mūṉṟum namavaru kālai
   vanta tokkum oṟṟiyal nilaiyē.
   ituvum mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvāmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aintum mūṉṟum nama varukālai-ainteṉṉu meṇṇum mūṉṟeṉṉumeṇṇum nakara
   mutaṉmoḻiyum makara mutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, oṟṟiyal
   nilai-nilaimoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟu naṭakkum nilaimaikūṟiṉ, vantatu
   okkum-mēṟ kūṟiyavāṟē makaramum pakaramumākātu varumoḻi mutal vanta
   oṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāy muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: ainnāḻi aimmaṇṭai munnāḻi mummaṇṭai eṉa varum.
   mūṉṟum aintum eṉṉāta muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāl nāṉāḻi nāṉ maṇṭai eṉpuḻi
   nilaimoḻi ṉakaram ṟakaramākātu niṉṟavāṟē niṉṟu muṭitalum varumoḻi
   mutaṉiṉṟa nakaram ṉakaramākat tiriya nilaimoḻi nakaraṅ keṭutaluṅ koḷka.
   (46)
   ----------
   "mūṉṟu" "nāṉku" "aintu" muṉ vakaramutaṉ moḻi
   452. mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakaram varuvaḻit
   tōṉṟiya vakarat turuvā kummē.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu-mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu, vakaram
   varuvaḻi-vakaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, tōṉṟiya vakarattu
   uruvākum-avvarumoḻiyāyt tōṉṟiya vakarattiṉ vaṭivāy muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: muvvaṭṭi eṉa varum.
   tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉē mutal nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟiṉṟi mūvaṭṭi eṉṟumām. (47)
   -----------
   453. nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: nāṉkaṉoṟṟu-nāṉkāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu,
   lakāramākum-vakaramutaṉmoḻi vantāl lakara oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum;
   e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: nālvaṭṭi eṉa varum. (48)
   -----------
   454. ainta ṉoṟṟē muntaiyatu keṭumē.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: aintaṉoṟṟu-aintāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu, muntaiyatu
   keṭum-vakaramutaṉmoḻi vantāl muṉṉiṉṟa vaṭivu keṭṭu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: aivaṭṭi eṉa varum
   muntai yeṉṟataṉāl nakara oṟṟuk keṭātu vakara oṟṟākat tirintu
   aivvaṭṭiyeṉac ciṟupāṉmai varum. (49)
   ------------
   'oru' 'iru' muṉ uyirmutaṉ moḻi
   455. mutalī reṇṇiṉ muṉ uyirvaru kālait
   tavaleṉa moḻipa ukarak kiḷavi
   mutaṉilai nīṭa lāvayi ṉāṉa.
   itu māṭṭēṟṟāṉ1 eytiya ukarattiṟkuk kēṭukūṟi mutaṉīḷka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu
   vilakkip piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ: mutalīreṇṇiṉ muṉ uyir varukālai - oru iru eṉa muṭintuniṉṟa
   iraṇṭeṇṇiṉ muṉṉar uyir mutaṉ moḻi varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu,
   ukarakkiḷavi tavaleṉa moḻipa - nilaimoḻi yukaramākiya eḻuttuk
   keṭutalāmeṉṟu colluvar pulavar. āvayiṉāṉa mutaṉilai nīṭal -
   avviraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ nīṇṭu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e - ṭu : ōrakal īrakal ōruḻakku īruḻakku eṉvarum. (50)
   -------------
   1 māṭṭēṟu 'aḷantaṟikiḷaviyum' (41) eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiyatu.

   ---------
   "mūṉṟu' 'nāṉku' 'aintu' muṉ uyirmutaṉ moḻi.
   456. mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyun
   tōṉṟiya vakarat tiyaṟkai yākum.
   ituvum atu.
   i - ḷ : mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyum - mūṉṟeṉṉumeṇṇum nāṉkeṉṉumeṇṇum
   ainteṉṉumeṇṇum, tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟkaiyākum - muṉṉarttōṉṟi niṉṟa
   vakaram varumoḻikkuk kūṟiya iyalpāka mūṉṟiṉkaṇ vakara oṟṟāyum,
   nāṉkiṉkaṇ lakara oṟṟāyum, aintiṉkaṇ oṟṟukkeṭṭum muṭiyum; e - ṟu.
   e - ṭu : muvvakal muvvuḻakku eṉa itaṟkut tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāl oṟṟiraṭṭutal
   koḷka. nālakal nāluḻakku aiyakal aiyuḻakku eṉavarum.
   tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāl mēl mūṉṟeṉpatu mutal nīṇṭa iṭattu nilaimoḻi
   ṉakaravoṟṟuk keṭuttukkoḷka. iyaṟkai yeṉṟataṉāl toṭarmoḻikkaṇ
   oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. 'mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē' mutaliya mūṉṟu cūttiramuṅ koṇarntu
   muṭikka. (51)
   avaṟṟuḷ 'mūṉṟu' eṉpataṟku, mēlum oru muṭipu.
   -----------
   457. mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉilai nīṭalu murittē
   uḻakkeṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkat tāṉa.
   itu muṉṉark kuṟukumeṉṟataṉai nīṇṭumuṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.
   i - ḷ : mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉilai nīṭalum urittu – mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa
   eḻuttu nīṇṭumuṭitalum urittu, aḵti yāṇṭeṉiṉ, uḻakku eṉ kiḷavi
   vaḻakkattāṉa-uḻakkeṉṉuñ col muṭiyum vaḻakkiṭattu; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: mūvuḻakku eṉa varum.
   vaḻakkattāṉa eṉpataṉāṉ akaleṉkiḷavikku mutaṉilai nīṭaluṅ koḷka. mūvakal
   eṉa varum. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē nilaimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟuk keṭukka.
   mūḻakku mūḻākkeṉṉum marumuṭipu ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ muṭikka. (52)
   -------------
   "āṟu" eṉpataṟkum am muṭipu
   458. āṟeṉ kiḷavi mutalnī ṭummē.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: āṟeṉ kiḷavi-āṟeṉṉu meṇṇuppeyar akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa variṉ, mutal
   nīṭum-muṉṉark kuṟuki niṉṟa mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   aṟuveṉṉātu āṟeṉṟār. tirintataṉṟiripatu eṉṉum nayattāl.
   e-ṭu: āṟakal āṟuḻakku eṉa varum. (53)
   ---------
   "oṉpatu" eṉṉuñ col
   459. oṉpā ṉiṟuti urupunilai tiriyā
   tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyai moḻiyē.
   itu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭātu niṉṟu iṉ peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu
   vilakkip piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: oṉpāṉ iṟuti urupunilai tiriyātu-aḷavum niṟaiyum varuvaḻi
   oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟukarantaṉvaṭivu nilaitiriyātu niṉṟu,
   cāriyaimoḻi iṉpeṟalvēṇṭum- cāriyaiccollākiya iṉ peṟṟu muṭitalai
   virumpum āciriyaṉ; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: oṉpatiṉkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi akal uḻakku
   eṉavum, kaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum.
   cāriyaimoḻi yeṉṟataṉāṉ iṉṉōṭu ukaramum valleḻuttuṅ koṭuttuc ceykaiceytu
   muṭikka. oṉpatiṟṟukkalam cāṭi eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.
   urupeṉpataṉāṉ oṉpatiṟṟeṉa oṟṟiraṭṭutal ellāvaṟṟiṟkuṅkoḷka.
   iṉṉum itaṉāṉē oṉpati ṉāḻiyeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakarak kēṭuṅ koḷka.
   "aḷavākumoḻi mutal" (eḻu-121) eṉpataṉāṉum "nilaiiya" eṉṉum ilēcāṉum
   iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka. (54)
   -----------
   oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu eṇmuṉ nūṟu
   460. nūṟumuṉ variṉuṅ kūṟiya iyalpē.
   iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉpataṉaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: muṉ-oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, nūṟu variṉum-nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇuppeyar
   vantālum, kūṟiya iyalpu-mēṟ patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumvaḻikkūṟiya iyalpu
   eyti muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   atu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu mūṉṟumā āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki mutalīreṇṇi
   ṉoṟṟu rakaramāy ukaram peṟṟu iṭainilai rakaram iraṇṭaṉkaṭ keṭṭu
   muṭitalām.
   e-ṭu: orunūṟu irunūṟu aṟunūṟu eṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. ivai māṭṭēṟṟāṉ
   muṭintaṉa. māṭṭēṟu ovvātaṉa mēṟkūṟi muṭippa. (55)
   --------------
   ataṉkaṇ "mūṉṟu" eṉṉuñ col
   461. mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāra mākum.
   itu māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē nakāramākum-mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉ kaṇiṉṟa ṉakaravoṟṟu
   nakaravoṟṟākum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: munnūṟu eṉa varum. (56)
   ------------
   ataṉkaṇ "nāṉku" "aintu"
   462. nāṉkum aintum oṟṟumey tiriyā.
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu mey tiriyā-nāṉkeṉṉu meṇṇum ainteṉṉumeṇṇun
   tammoṟṟukkaḷ nilaitiriyātu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: nāṉūṟu ainnūṟu eṉa varum.
   meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ nāṉūṟeṉpuḻi varumoḻi nakarattuḷ ūkāram pirittu "laṉaveṉa
   varūum" (eḻu-149) eṉpataṉāṉ ṉakaravoṟṟākki ūkāramēṟṟi nilaimoḻi ṉakaraṅ
   keṭuttuk koḷka. (57)
   -------------
   ataṉkaṇ "oṉpatu" eṉṉuñ col.
   463. oṉpāṉ mutaṉilai muntukiḷan taṟṟē
   muntai yoṟṟē ṉakāram iraṭṭum
   nūṟeṉ kiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa
   ūā vākum iyaṟkait teṉpa
   āyiṭai varutal ikāra rakāram
   īṟumey keṭuttu makara moṟṟum.1
   ituvum atu.
   i-ḷ: oṉpāṉ mutaṉilai muntu kiḷantaṟṟu-oṉpa teṉṉumeṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa okaram
   mēṟ patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumvaḻik kūṟiyavāṟu pōla oru takaram oṟṟi
   ataṉmēl ēṟimuṭiyum, muntai oṟṟē ḷakāram iraṭṭum-avvokarattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa
   ṉakara oṟṟu ḷakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, nūṟeṉkiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa ū
   āvākum iyaṟkait teṉpa-varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉṉumeṇṇuppeyar nakaramākiya
   meykeṭa ataṉmēl ēṟiya ūkāram ākāramām iyalpaiyuṭaitteṉpar pulavar,
   āyiṭai ikara rakaram varutal -ammoḻiyiṭai ōr ikaramum rakāramum varuka,
   īṟu mey keṭuttu makaram oṟṟum-īṟākiya kuṟṟukarattiṉaiyum aḵtu ēṟiniṉṟa
   ṟakara oṟṟiṉaiyum keṭuttu ōr makara oṟṟu vantu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   meyyeṉpataṉāṉ nilaimoḻikkaṭ pakaraṅ keṭukka.
   e-ṭu: toḷḷāyiram eṉa varum.
   itaṉai oṉpateṉṉum okarattiṉmuṉṉar vanta takara oṟṟiṉmēlē
   okarattaiyēṟṟip pakaraṅ keṭuttuk kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu niṉṟa
   ṉakara oṟṟiṉai iraṇṭu ḷakara oṟṟākki nūṟeṉpataṉ nakaraṅkeṭuttu ūkāram
   ākāramākki ḷakarattiṉ mēlēṟṟi ikaramum rakaramum varuvittu vikārappaṭṭa
   uyirākiya ākārattiṉmuṉ uṭampaṭumey yakāram varuvittu ṟakara ukaraṅ
   keṭuttu makara oṟṟu varuvittu muṭikka. (58)
   -------------
   1 māṭṭēṟu 'aḷantaṟikiḷaviyum' (41) eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟ kūṟiyatu.

   ------------
   oṉṟu "iraṇṭu" muṉ āyiram
   464. āyirak kiḷavi varūuṅ kālai
   mutalī reṇṇiṉ ukaraṅ keṭumē.
   iḵtu avvoṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: āyirakkiḷavi varūuṅ kālai-āyirameṉṉuñ col oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ
   varuṅkālattu, mutal īreṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭum-oru iru eṉṉum iraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṭ
   peṟṟu niṉṟa ukaraṅ keṭṭu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   ukaraṅkeṭumeṉavē ēṉaiyaṉa muṉṉark kūṟiyavāṟē niṟṟal peṟṟām.
   e-ṭu: orāyiram irāyiram eṉa varum. (59)
   in nūṟpā, oṉpatu+nūṟu=toḷḷāyiram eṉap puṇarkkiṉṟatu. "oṉpāṉokaramicai"
   (eḻu-445) eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟkuk kūṟiya aṭikkuṟippai itaṟkuṅ koḷka.
   ----------------
   avaṟṟiṟku mēlum oru muṭipu
   465. mutaṉilai nīṭiṉum māṉa millai.
   iḵtu eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: mutaṉilai nīṭiṉum māṉamillai-ammutalīreṇṇiṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa okāra
   ikāraṅkaḷ nīṇṭumuṭiyiṉuṅ kuṟṟamillai; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: ōrāyiram īrāyiram eṉa varum. (60)
   -------------
   "mūṉṟu" muṉ āyiram
   466. mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakāra mākum.
   itu mūṉṟeṉṉumeṇ āyirattōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vakāramākum-mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu vakara
   oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: muvvāyiram eṉa varum.
   muṉṉiṟ cūttirattu nilai eṉṟataṉāṉ itaṉai mutaṉilai nīṭṭi vakara oṟṟuk
   keṭuttu mūvāyiram eṉa muṭikka. (61)
   --------------
   "nāṉku" muṉ āyiram
   467. nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.
   itu nāṉkeṉṉumeṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: nāṉkaṉoṟṟē lakāramākum-nāṉkeṉṉumeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakara
   oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: nālāyiram eṉa varum. (62)
   -----------
   "aintu" muṉ āyiram
   468. ainta ṉoṟṟē yakāra mākum.
   iḵtu ainteṉṉumeṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē yakāramākum-ainteṉṉu meṇṇiṉka ṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu yakara
   oṟṟākat tirintu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   aiyāyiram eṉa varum. (63)
   -------------
   "āṟu" muṉ āyiram
   469. āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaram
   īṟumey oḻiyak keṭutal vēṇṭum.
   iḵtu āṟeṉṉu meṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram-āṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉka ṇiṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram
   neṭumutal kuṟuki aṟuveṉa muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ, mey oḻiya īṟu keṭutal
   vēṇṭum- atu tāṉēṟiya meyyākiya ṟakara oṟṟuk keṭātu niṟpa
   muṟṟukaramākiya īṟu tāṉ keṭṭup puṇartalai virumpum āciriyaṉ; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: aṟāyiram eṉa varum.
   muṉṉar "neṭumutal kuṟukum" (eḻu-440) eṉṟa vaḻi aṟuveṉa niṉṟa
   muṟṟukarattiṟkē īṇṭuk kēṭu kūṟiṉāreṉpatu peṟṟām. eṉṉai?
   kuṟṟiyalukaramāyiṉ ēṟimuṭitaliṉ. itu kuṟṟukaran tirintu muṟṟukaramāy
   niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu muṭipukūṟiṉār. muṟṟiyalukaram īṟumeyyoḻiyak keṭumeṉavē
   kuṟṟukaraṅ keṭātu ēṟimuṭiyu meṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṟ peṟutum. āṟāyiram
   eṉa varum.
   maruṅkeṉṟataṉāṟ piṟaporuṭ peyarkkaṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟukātu niṉṟu muṭital
   koḷka. āṟākuvatē eṉa varum. (64)
   ------------
   "oṉpatu" muṉ "āyiram"
   470. oṉpā ṉiṟuti urupunilai tiriyā
   tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyai marapē.
   iḵtu oṉpateṉṉumeṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: oṉpāṉ iṟuti- oṉpateṉṉu meṇṇiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟukaram, uruvunilai
   tiriyātu-taṉ vaṭivunilai tirintu keṭātu, cāriyai marapu iṉ peṟal
   vēṇṭum- cāriyaiyākiya marapiṉaiyuṭaiya iṉ peṟṟa muṭitalai āciriyaṉ;
   e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉa varum.
   uruveṉṟum nilaiyeṉṟuñ cāriyai marapeṉṟuṅkūṟiya mikaiyāl āyiramallāta
   piṟaveṇṇiṉkaṇṇum poruṭ peyariṭattum iṉṉum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu
   muṭiyum muṭipu koḷka. oṉpatiṟṟuk kōṭi1 oṉpatiṟṟoṉpatu
   oṉpatiṟṟuttaṭakkai oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu eṉa varum.
   iṉṉum ivvilēcāṉē vēṟoru muṭipiṉmaiyiṟ kūṟātoḻinta eṇṇāyira meṉṟavaḻi
   oṟṟiraṭṭutalum īṇṭuk kūṟiyavaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutaluṅ koḷka. "aḷavāku
   moḻimutal" (eḻu-121) eṉpataṉuḷ "nilaiiya" eṉṟataṉāṉ ṉakaram
   ṟakaramātal2 koḷka. (65)
   ---------
   1 ilakkam kōṭiyeṉṉum eṇṇup peyarkaḷ ivviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭāmaiyāl paḻan
       tamiḻarkku 99999 varaitāṉ eṇṇat terintiruntatu eṉṟu cilar koḷvar.
       ilakkam kōṭi eṉpaṉa kuṟṟiyalukarac coṟkaḷalla veṉṟum, kuṟṟukarac
       coṟkaḷē ivviyaliṟ puṇarttuk kāṭṭappaṭuvaṉa veṉṟum, ilakkattiṉ
       maṟupeyarāṉa nūṟāyiram eṉṉum collum pattu nūṟāyiram varai
       puṇarttaṟkiṭamum aṭutta (eḻu-471) nūṟpāviṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷaveṉṟum,
       ilakkam kōṭi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ muṟaiyē periya ilakkama kaṭaiciyeṉa
       eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum taṉit tamiḻc coṟkaḷē eṉṟum aṟintu koḷka.
       2 ṉakaram ṟakaramātal oṉpatiṟṟukkōṭi oṉpatiṟṟoṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟil.

   -----------
   oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu eṇ muṉ "nūṟāyiram"
   471. nūṟā yiramuṉ varūuṅ kālai
   nūṟa ṉiyaṟkai mutaṉilaik kiḷavi.
   iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉṉumeṇ aṭaiyaṭutta āyiram muṭiyumāṟu
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: nūṟāyiram muṉ varūuṅ kālai-nūṟāyirameṉṉum aṭaiyaṭuttamoḻi
   oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, mutaṉilaik kiḷavi
   nūṟaṉ iyaṟkai- oṉṟeṉṉum mutaṉilaik kiḷavi oṉṟu muṉ nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇōṭu
   muṭintāṟpōla vikārameyti muṭiyum. eṉavē vaḻi nilaik kiḷaviyākiya iraṇṭu
   mutaliya eṇkaḷ vikārameytiyum eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: oru nūṟāyiram eṉa varum. ēṉaiyaṉa irunūṟāyiram iraṇṭu nūṟāyiram,
   munnūṟāyiram mūṉṟu nūṟāyiram, nāṉūṟāyiram nāṉkunūṟāyiram ainnūṟāyiram
   aintu nūṟāyiram, aṟunūṟāyirama āṟu nūṟāyiram, eṇṇūṟāyiram eṭṭu
   nūṟāyiram, oṉpatunūṟāyiram eṉavarum. ivvikārap paṭṭaṉavaṟṟiṟkuk
   kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu mutalīreṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaramākki ukaram
   varuvittu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukki mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu nakāramākki nāṉkum
   aintum oṟṟumeytiriyā vākki eṭṭa ṉoṟṟu ṇakāramākki ilēcukaḷāṟkoṇṭa
   ceykaikaḷil vēṇṭuvaṉavuṅ koṇarntu muṭikka.
   ēṟpuḻikkōṭa leṉpataṉāl toḷḷāyirameṉṟa muṭipuṉōṭu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟatēṉum
   avvāṟu muṭiyāteṉṟu koḷka. muṉṉeṉpataṉāṉ iṉ cāriyai peṟṟu oṉpatiṉāyiram
   eṉṟumām. nilaiyeṉṟataṉāṉ mūṉṟum āṟum iyalpāka muṭivuḻi neṭumutal
   kuṟukāmai koḷka. (66)
   --------
   nūṟu muṉ oṉṟu mutaloṉpatu eṇkaḷ
   472. nūṟeṉ kiḷavi oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ
   kīṟuciṉai yoḻiya iṉavoṟṟu mikumē.
   itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi-nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇuppeyar, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku-oṉṟumutal
   oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, īṟu ciṉaiyoḻiya-īṟākiya kuṟṟukaran taṉṉāṟ
   paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyoṭuṅ keṭātuniṟpa, iṉa oṟṟu mikum-acciṉaikku iṉamākiya
   ṟakara oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa varum. iraṇṭumutal oṉpatu aḷavuñ ceykai yaṟintu
   oṭṭuka.
   īṟuciṉai eṉṟu ōtiya mikaiyāṉ nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu piṟa eṇṇum piṟaporuṭ peyarum
   ivvitiyum piṟavitiyum eytimuṭital koḷka. nūṟṟuppattu nūṟṟukkōṭi
   nūṟṟuttoṇṇūṟu eṉavum, nūṟṟukkuṟai nūṟṟitaḻttāmarai nūṟṟukkāṇam
   nūṟṟukkāṉmaṇṭapam eṉavum iṉa oṟṟu mikkaṉa koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē
   irunūṟṟoṉṟu iraṇṭu nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa nūṟu aṭaiyaṭuttavaḻiyuṅ koḷka. (67)
   ------------
   nūṟu muṉ "orupaḵtu" mutaliyaṉa.
   473. avaiyūr pattiṉum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.
   iḵtu annūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ aṭaiyaṭutta vaḻip puṇarumāṟu
   kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: avai ūrpattiṉum-annūṟeṉpatu niṉṟu muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷai
   ūrntu vanta patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattum, attoḻiṟṟākum-īṟu ciṉaiyoḻiya
   iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: nūṟṟorupaḵtu irupaḵtu muppaḵtu nāṟpaḵtu aimpaḵtu aṟupaḵtu
   eḻupaḵtu eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum. maṟṟu nūṟṟoṉpatu avai ūrappaṭṭu vantatu
   aṉmai uṇarka.
   ākumeṉṟataṉāṉ orunūṟṟorupaḵtu irunūṟṟorupaḵtu eṉa nilaimoḻi aṭaiyaṭuttu
   muṭiyum muṭipuṅ koḷka. (68)
   ----------
   nūṟu muṉ aḷavuppeyar niṟaippeyar
   474. aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyātu
   kuṟṟiya lukaramum valleḻut tiyaṟkaiyum
   muṟkiḷan taṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar.
   itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyātu-nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum
   niṟaippeyarum puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṟ ṟiriyātu iṉa oṟṟu
   mikkumuṭiyum, kuṟṟiyalukaramum valleḻuttiyaṟkaiyum-avviṭattuk
   kuṟṟiyalukaraṅ keṭāmaiyum iṉa oṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭarmoḻiyāy niṟṟaliṉ
   varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum, muṟkiḷantaṉṉa eṉmaṉār
   pulavar-"valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikumē" (eḻu-426) eṉa
   vaṉṟoṭarmoḻikkuk kūṟiya taṉmaiyavāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: nūṟṟukkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi akal uḻakku
   eṉavum, kaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. tiriyāteṉṟataṉāl nūṟeṉpatu
   aṭaiyaṭutta vaḻiyum ivviti koḷka. aḵtu orunūṟṟukkalam irunūṟṟukkalam
   eṉa varum. (69)
   -----------
   "oru paḵtu" mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ muṉ oṉṟu mutal oṉpatu eṇkaḷ
   475. oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
   oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭai mikumē
   niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum.
   iḵtu oṉṟumutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭaiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭum oṉṟumutal
   oṉpāṉkaḷaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi-oṉṟumutal eṭṭu īṟākap patteṉṉum eṇ
   ēṟi orucollāki niṉṟa oru paḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷ, oṉṟumutal
   oṉpāṟku-oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ varumoḻiyāy vantu puṇarum iṭattu, niṉṟa
   āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum-paḵteṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭṭu muṭitalai
   virumpum āciriyaṉ, oṟṟu iṭaimikum- āṇṭu iṉa oṟṟākiya oru takara oṟṟu
   iṭaimikku muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: orupattoṉṟu irupattoṉṟu orupattiraṇṭu irupattiraṇṭu eṉa
   ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ orupattoṉṟu orupattiraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇkaḷ
   atikārattāl niṉṟa nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aṭuttu varumeṉṟu1 uṇarka. (70)
   ----------
   1 nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aṭuttuvarutal-nūṟṟorupattoṉṟu nūṟṟorupattiraṇṭu eṉa
       varutal.

   -------------
   avaṟṟiṉmuṉ "āyiram"
   476. āyiram variṉē iṉṉeṉ cāriyai
   āvayi ṉoṟṟiṭai mikuta lillai.
   iḵtu orupaḵtu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu āyirattaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: āyiram variṉ iṉṉeṉ cāriyai-avvoṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi
   āyirattōṭu puṇarum iṭattu iṉcāriyai peṟum, āvayiṉ oṟṟu iṭai mikutal
   illai- avviṭattut takara oṟṟu iṭaivantu mikātu; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: orupatiṉāyiram irupatiṉāyiram eṉa eṇpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka. ivai
   nūṟṟorupatiṉāyiram eṉavu varum.
   āvayiṉ eṉṟataṉāl nūṟāyirattorupattīrāyiram eṉṟāṟpōla attup peṟutalum
   piṟavuṅ koḷka. (71)
   -------------
   avaṟṟiṉmuṉ aḷavup peyar niṟaip peyar
   477. aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyā.
   iḵtu oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum
   puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyā-orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar
   aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum vantāl oṟṟu iṭaimikātu iṉcāriyai peṟṟu
   muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: orupatiṉkalam cāṭi tūtai pāṉai nāḻi maṇṭai vaṭṭi akal uḻakku
   eṉavum, orupatiṉkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟiṟku nūṟu
   aṭaiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka.
   tiriyā eṉṟataṉāṉ orupatiṟṟukkalam irupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉun
   toṭakkattaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ iṉṉiṉ akaram ṟakaramākat tirintu iraṭṭutalum
   ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟutaluṅ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē orupatiṉāḻi
   eṉṟavaḻi varumoḻi nakaran tirintuḻi nilaimoḻi ṉakarakkēṭuṅ koḷka.
   aḷavu niṟaiyu mataṉō raṉṉa eṉṟu pāṭam ōtuvār muṉṉarc cūttirattu
   "āvayiṉ" eṉṟataṉāṉum ataṉ muṉṉarc cūttirattu "niṉṟa" eṉṟataṉāṉum
   ivaṟṟai muṭippār. (72)
   -------------
   oṉṟumutal eṇmuṉ poruṭ peyar
   478. mutaṉilai eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum
   ñanaman tōṉṟiṉum yavavan tiyaiyiṉum
   mutaṉilai yiyaṟkai eṉmaṉār pulavar.
   iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyaraip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: mutaṉilai eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum- oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ
   valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi variṉum, ñanama tōṉṟiṉum-ñanamakkaḷākiya
   melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum; yavavantu iyaiyiṉum-yavakkaḷākiya iṭai
   yeḻuttu mutaṉ moḻi variṉum, mutaṉilai iyaṟkai eṉmaṉār
   pulavar-avvoṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉeytiya muṭipu nilaimai eyti
   muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; e-ṟu.
   eṉavē vaḻinilaiyeṇṇākiya iraṇṭu mutalākiya eṇkaḷ ammūṉṟu kaṇamum
   mutaṉmoḻiyāy variṉ mutaṉilai muṭipāki vikāram eytiyum eytātu iyalpāyum
   muṭiyum.
   e-ṭu: orukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum, irukal
   iraṇṭukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum oṭṭuka.
   ivveṇkaḷiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭu mutalīreṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakāramāy
   ukaram vantatu, irukal mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṭainilai rakāraṅ keṭukka.
   mukkal mūṉṟukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.
   itaṟku neṭumutal kuṟukki "mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum" (eḻu-447) eṉpataṉāṉ
   muṭikka. muṉṉar eṇṇup peyarum aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum varuvaḻik
   kūṟiya vikāraṅkaḷiṟ poruṭpeyarkkum ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu muṭittu
   ellāvaṟṟiṟkum nilai yeṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟut tirintu muṭikka. avai mūṉṟaṟkum
   aintaṟkum ñakaram varuvaḻi ñakara oṟṟātalum mūṉṟaṟku yakaram varuvaḻi
   vakara oṟṟātalumām. nāṟkal nāṉkukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai, nāṉñāṇ nāṉkuñāṇ
   nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; aiṅkal aintukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai, aiññāṇ aintu ñāṇ
   nūl maṇi, aiyāḻ aintuyāḻ aivaṭṭu aintuvaṭṭu; aṟukal āṟukal cuṉai tuṭi
   paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; eṇkal eṭṭukkal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai eṇñāṇ
   eṭṭuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; oṉpatukal cuṉai tuṭi paṟai ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ
   vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉpatiṉkal eṉac ceṉṟatēṉum vaḻakkiṉmaiyiṉ oḻikka.
   iṉṉum māṭṭēṟiṉṟi varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām muṭipunilai yeṉṟataṉāṉ muṭikka.
   (73)
   ------------
   avaṟṟuṭaṉ uyir mutal moḻiyum yakaramum
   479. ataṉilai uyirkkum yāvaru kālai
   mutaṉilai okaram ōvā kummē
   rakarat tukaran tuvarak keṭumē.
   iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyaruḷ uyirmutaṉ moḻi muṭiyumāṟum
   mēṟkūṟiya yakāram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: mutaṉilaikku-oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ tiripākiya oru eṉṉum eṇṇiṟku, uyirum
   yāvum varukālai -uyir mutaṉmoḻiyum yāmutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy
   varuṅkālattu, ataṉ nilai-ammutaṉilaiyiṉ taṉmai ivvāṟām, okaram
   ōvākum-okaram ōkāramāy nīḷum, rakarattu ukaran tuvarak keṭum-rakarattu
   mēṉiṉṟa ukaram muṟṟakkeṭṭu muṭiyum; e-ṟu.
   nāṉkāvataṉai mutaṉilaiyōṭu kūṭṭi ataṉkaṇ niṉṟa ummaiyai uyirōṭum
   yāvōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. eṉavē vaḻinilai yeṇkaḷ uyir mutaṉ moḻi vanta iṭattu
   muṟkūṟiyavāṟē iruvāṟṟāṉum muṭiyum.
   e-ṭu: ōraṭai ōrākam ōrilai ōrīṭṭam ōrulai ōrūcal ōreḻu ōrēṭu ōraiyam
   ōroḻuṅku ōrōlai ōrauviyam eṉa varum. kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭuttu
   mutaleṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakaramākkuka. ōr yāḻ ōr yāṉai eṉa varum. tuvara
   eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṇṭeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ikarattai nīṭṭi rakarattuḷ ukarattaik
   keṭuttu īracai īryāṉai eṉavum, mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ṉakaravoṟṟuk keṭuttu
   mūvacai mūyāṉai eṉavum muṭikka. ivai ceyyuṇ muṭipu. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē
   iṅṅaṉam varuvaṉa piṟavum aṟintu muṭittuk koḷka. (74)
   -------------
   iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu eṇmuṉ 'mā' eṉṉuñ col.
   480. iraṇṭumuta loṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar
   vaḻaṅkiyal māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
   makara aḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē.
   iḵtu iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavu mutaliya
   mūṉṟaṟkum uriya māveṉpatu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: iḵtu iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar - iraṇṭeṉṉumeṇ mutalāka
   oṉpateṉṉumeṇ īṟāka niṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vaḻaṅku iyal mā eṉ
   kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ - vaḻakkiṉ kaṇṇē naṭanta aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uriya mā
   veṉṉuñ col varumoḻiyāy variṉ, makara aḷavoṭu nikaralum urittu -
   avveṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tantu puṇarkkappaṭum maṇṭaiyeṉṉum aḷavup
   peyarōṭu ottu vikārappaṭṭu muṭitalum urittu; ummaiyāṉ vikārappaṭātu
   iyalpāy muṭitalum urittu; e - ṟu.
   vaḻakkiyal vaḻaṅkiyaleṉa vikāram, makara aḷavu makaramutaṉ moḻiyākiya
   aḷavuppeyareṉap paṇputtokai. aḵtu 'aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum' (eḻu - 446)
   eṉpataṉuḷ oru maṇṭai eṉa muṭittatām.
   e - ṭu : irumā mummā nāṉmā aimmā aṟumā eṇmā oṉpatiṉmā eṉa muṉṉarkkūṟiya
   cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ vikārappaṭutti muṭikka. iṉi ummaiyaṉ vikārappaṭuttātu
   iraṇṭumā mūṉṟumā nāṉkumā aintumā āṟumā eṭṭumā oṉpatumā eṉṟu muṭikka.
   puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ 'aḷavuniṟaiyum' (eḻu - 389) eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ
   neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram vantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟiṉār. ataṉāṉ īṇṭu eḻumā eṉa
   muṭikka, ēḻmāveṉa muṭital vaḻakkiṉṟu.
   iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉeṉṟu eṭuttōtiṉamaiyuṉ, oṉṟaṟku orumāveṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi
   oṉṟumā veṉṉum muṭipu illaiyāyiṟṟu.
   vaḻaṅkiyaṉmā eṉṟār vilaṅkumāvai nīkkutaṟku. (75)
   -----------

                       9.6. atikārap puṟaṉaṭai

   puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ oḻuntuniṉṟa meyyīṟukaḷiṉ ceyyuḷ muṭipu
   481. laṉaveṉa varūvum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
   ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭap piṟavum
   aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭait tōṉṟic
   ceyyuṭ ṭoṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa nilaiyum
   vēṟṟumai kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.
   itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ oḻintuniṉṟa ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i - ḷ : laṉa eṉa varūvum puḷḷi iṟuti muṉ – lakāra ṉakārameṉṟu kūṟa
   varukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟṟuc coṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭa-ummeṉṉuñ
   cāriyaiyuṅ keḻuveṉṉuñ cāriyaiyum uṭpaṭa, piṟavum aṉṉa marapiṉ
   moḻiyiṭaittōṉṟi-piṟacāriyaiyum appeṟṟippaṭṭa marapiṉaiyuṭaiya
   moḻiyiṭattē tōṉṟi, ceyyuḷ toṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa nilaiyum-ceyyuṭcoṟkaḷait
   toṭarpu paṭuttik kūṟumiṭattup poruḷpaṭa niṟkum. vēṟṟumai kuṟitta
   poruḷvayiṉāṉa-vēṟṟumaiyaik kuṟitta poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: "vāṉavari villun tiṅkaḷum pōlum" itaṟku ummeṉṉuñ cāriyaiyiṉ
   makarattai "ammiṉiṟuti" (eḻu-129) eṉṉuñ cūttirattuḷ "taṉmey" eṉṟataṉāṟ
   piṟacāriyaiyan tiriyumeṉa nakara oṟṟākki nilaimoḻi lakara oṟṟiṉmēl
   uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. villuntiṅkaḷum pōlumeṉpataṟku villiṭaittiṅkaḷ
   pōlumeṉa ēḻaṉurupu virittup poruḷuraikka. "kalkeḻu kāṉavar nalkuṟu
   makaḷē: (kuṟun-71) itaṟkuk kallaik keḻīiyaṉa eṉa uraikka.
   "mānitikkiḻavaṉum pōṉm" (akam- 66) itaṟkuk kiḻavaṉaippōṉmeṉa uraikka,
   iv vummai ciṟappaṉṟu. "kāṉkeḻunaṭu" (akam-98) itaṟkuk kāṉaikkeḻīiya eṉa
   uraikka.
   iṉi aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyiṭai eṉṟataṉāṟ keḻuveṉṟatu piṟa colliṭattē
   "paṇaikeḻu peruntiṟaṟ palvēṉmaṉṉar" "tuṟaikeḻu māntai" (naṟṟiṇai-35)
   eṉa iyalpāka varuvaṉavum, "vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar" (akam-17) "payaṅkeḻu
   māmaḻai" (paṟam-266) eṉa nilaimoḻi yīṟṟeḻuttut tiriya varuvaṉavuṅ
   koḷka.
   iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iccāriyaiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekara nīṭciyuṅ koḷka. "pūkkēḻ
   toṭalai nuṭaṅka veḻuntu " (akam-28) "tuṟaikēḻūraṉ koṭumai nāṇi"
   (aiṅkuṟu-11) ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāvatum ēṟpuḻi mūṉṟāvatum virikka. "ceṅkēḻ
   meṉkoṭi" (akam-80) eṉpuḻik keḻuveṉṉum uriccol "eḻuttup pirinticaittal"
   (col-395) eṉpataṉāṉ nīṇṭateṉṟu uṇarka. mey yeṉṟataṉāṟ pūkkēḻeṉpuḻi
   valloṟṟu mikutal koḷka.
   iṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṭ piṟacāriyai peṟṟu vikāraṅkaḷ eyti
   muṭivaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām iccūttiramē vitiyāka muṭittuk koḷka. (76)
   ---------
   eḻuttatikārattiṟ puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ.
   482. uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yākik
   kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum icaiyiṉun tōṉṟi
   neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟaicacoṟ kiḷaviyum
   uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai āyiru maruṅkiṉ
   aimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyuñ
   ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyiṉ
   meyyoruṅ kiyalun toḻiltoku moḻiyum
   tammiyal kiḷappiṉ tammuṉ tāmvarūum
   eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭap piṟavum
   aṉṉavai yellām maruviṉ pāttiya
   puṇariyal nilaiyiṭai uṇarat tōṉṟā.
   iḵtu ivvatikārattiṟ puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ ivaiyeṉa avaṟṟai eṭuttu
   uṇarttukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki-kūṟuṅkāl uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāka niṟpatōr
   collāki, kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum icaiyiṉun tōṉṟi-kuṟippiṉāṉum paṇpiṉāṉum
   icaiyiṉāṉum piṟantu, neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟaiccoṟ kiḷaviyum- oruvaḻippaṭa
   vārāta coṟṟaṉmai kuṟainta coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai
   āyiru maruṅkiṉ -uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai yeṉṉum avviraṇṭiṭattum uḷavākiya,
   aimpālaṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum-oruvaṉ orutti palar -oṉṟu pala eṉṉum
   aintupāliṉaiyum aṟitaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya paṇpukoḷpeyar tokun
   tokaiccollum, ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyiṉ-ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉum
   peyareccac coṟkaḷiṉuṭaiya, mey oruṅku iyalun toḻil tokumoḻiyum
   kālaṅkāṭṭum ummum akaramum orucoṟ kaṇṇē cēra naṭakkum puṭaipeyarcci
   tokku niṟkuñ coṟkaḷum, tamiyal kiḷappiṉ-tamatu taṉmai kūṟumiṭattu, tam
   muṉ tām varūum eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭa-niṟuttacolluṅ kuṟittuvaru
   kiḷaviyumāy vārātu tammuṉṉar tāmē vantu niṟkum eṇṇuppeyariṉatu
   tokutiyum uḷappaṭa. aṉṉa piṟavum ellām-attaṉmaiyākiya piṟavumellām,
   maruviṉ pāttiya-ulakattu maruvi naṭanta vaḻakkiṉatu pakutiyait tam
   ilakkaṇa mākavuṭaiya, puṇariyal nilai iṭai yuṇarattōṉṟā- oṉṟaṭōṉoṉṟu
   puṇarutal naṭanta taṉmai iṭam viḷaṅkat tōṉṟā; e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kaṇ viṇṇaviṇaittatu viṇviṇaittatu ivai kuṟippuric col; āṭai veḷḷa
   viḷarttatu veḷviḷarttatu ivai paṇpuriccol; kaṭal olla volittatu
   ollolittatu ivai icai yuriccol. "ollolinīr pāyvatē pōlun tuṟaivaṉ"
   eṉṟār ceyyuṭkaṇṇum. ivai uyirīṟāyum puḷḷiyīṟāyum niṟṟaliṉ oṉṟaṉkaṇ
   aṭakkalākāmaiyiṉ neṟippaṭavārā eṉṟār. viṇṇaviṇaittatu teṟipputtōṉṟat
   teṟitta teṉṟum viṇviṇaittatu teṟipputteṟitta teṉṟum ām. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum
   ivvāṟē uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam niṟṟaliṟ ṟaṉmai kuṟainta collāyiṟṟu. "viṉaiyē
   kuṟippē" (col-258) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya eṉa eṉpataṉai ivaṟṟōṭu
   kūṭṭiyavaḻi iṭaiccollātaliṉ viṇṇeṉaviṇaittatu eṉap puṇarkkappaṭumāṟu
   uṇarka.
   iṉik karumpārppāṉ karumpārppaṉi karumpārppār karuṅkutirai
   karuṅkutiraikaḷ eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuṭ kariyaṉākiya pārppāṉ kariyaḷākiya
   pārppaṉi kariyarākiya pārppār kariyatākiya kutirai kariyaṉavākiya
   kutiraikaḷ eṉa aimpāliṉaiyum uṇarttum paṇpukoḷpeyar tokkavāṟu kāṇka.
   ivaṟṟuṭ karumai eṉṉum paṇpuppeyar tokkatēṟ karumaiyākiya pārppāṉeṉa
   virittal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam viriyāmaiyiṟ paṇpukoḷ peyar tokkateṉṟu uṇarka.
   veṟṟilai veṟṟuppili veṟṟaṭi veṟṟeṉattoṭuttal eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavaṟṟuḷ
   veṟuvitākiya ilaiyeṉpatu pākkuṅ kōṭṭu nūṟuṅ kūṭātatāya paṇpuṇartti īṟu
   tokutaliṉ maruviṉ pāttiyavāy niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu. veṟuvitākiya
   uppiliyeṉṟatu ciṟitum uppiliyeṉa niṉṟatu. ēṉaiyavum aṉṉa. iṅṅaṉam
   aimpālun tokuttaṟku uriya mutaṉilaiyātaliṟ paṇarttalākāmai kūṟiṉār.
   iṉi āṭaraṅku ceykuṉṟu puṇarpoḻutu arivāḷ kolyāṉai celcelavu eṉa nilam
   mutalākiya peyareccantokka viṉaittokaikaḷai virikkuṅkāl, āṭiṉavaraṅku
   eṉac ceyta eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta akara īṟu iṟappu uṇarttiyum,
   āṭāniṉṟavaraṅku āṭumaraṅku eṉac ceyyumeṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta
   ummīṟu nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttiyum, avaṟṟāṉāya puṭaipeyarcciyait
   tōṟṟuvittu iraṇṭu peyareccamum orucoṟkaṇ oruṅku tokku niṟṟaliṉ ataṉai
   oru peyareccattiṉkaṇ aṭakkip puṇarkakalākāmaiyiṟ puṇarkkalākāteṉṟār.
   ummiṟuti nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttumāṟu "viṉaiyiṉtokuti" (col-415) eṉṉam
   eccaviyaṟ cūttirattuṭ kūṟutum. iv vum īṟu iraṇṭu kālamum
   oruṅkuṇarttutaṟ ciṟappu nōkkic ceyta eṉpataṉai āciriyar muṟkūṟārāyiṉar.
   iṉip pattu eṉa niṟuttip patteṉat tantu puṇarkkappaṭātu pappatteṉavum
   paḵpatteṉavum vaḻaṅkumāṟu uṇarka. orōvoṉṟeṉpatum atu. atutāṉē
   ōroṉṟōroṉṟākak koṭu eṉṟāṟ puṇarkkappaṭum.
   iṉi aṉṉapiṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉē uṇṭāṉeṉpuḻi uṇṇeṉṉum mutaṉilaiyuṅ
   kālaṅkāṭṭum ṭakaramum iṭaṉumpālum uṇarttum āṉum oṉṟaṭōṉoṉṟu
   puṇarkkappaṭā. avai niṟuttacolluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum aṉmaiyiṉ.
   kariyaṉeṉpuḻik karuveṉa niṟutti aṉṉeṉat tantu puṇarkkappaṭā, atu
   iṉṉaṉeṉṉum poruḷ tarutaliṉ.ēṉaiviṉaic coṟkaḷum ivvāṟē pirittup
   puṇarkkalākāmai uṇarka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē koḷḷeṉakkoṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻik
   koḷḷeṉpataṉai eṉa eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarkkappaṭāmaiyum ūraṉ veṟpaṉ mutaliya
   viṉaippeyar kaḷum1 piṟavum puṇarkkappaṭāmaiyum koḷka. ivvāciriyar
   puṇarkkap paṭāta iccoṟkaḷaiyum vaṭanūṟkaṇmuṭitta aṉakaṉ aṉapāyaṉ
   akaḷaṅkaṉ mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷaiyum piṉṉuḷḷōr2 muṭittal mutaṉūlōṭu
   māṟikoḷak kūṟalāmeṉṟu uṇarka. (77)
   ------
   1 ūraṉ veṟpaṉ eṉṉum tiṇaittalaivar peyarkaḷai viṉaippeyarkaḷeṉṟu
       iṅkuk kūṟiyiruppatu poruntātu.
       2 piṉṉuḷḷōr-naṉṉūlār ciṉṉūlār vīracōḻiyakārar mutaliyōr.

   ----------
   eḻuttatikārattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭai
   483. kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum
   vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
   viḷampiya iyaṟkaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ
   vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅki ṉuṇarntaṉar oḻukkal
   naṉmati nāṭṭat teṉmaṉār pulavar
   iḵtu iv vatikārattu eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟai yellām
   itaṉāṉē muṭikka eṉa atikārappuṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   i-ḷ: kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum-muṉṉar eṭuttōtappaṭṭaṉa allāta
   coṟkaḷ ceyyuḷiṭattut tirintu muṭivaṉavum, vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu
   tirinavum- nālvakai vaḻakkum naṭakkumiṭattu maruvutalōṭu tirintu muṭi
   vaṉavum, viḷampiya iyaṟkaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ- muṉṉark kūṟiya ilakkaṇa
   muṟaimaiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟumāyiṉ avaṟṟai, naṉmati nāṭṭattu-nalla
   aṟivi ṉatu ārāycciyālē, vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ-vaḻakku muṭintu
   naṭakkumiṭattē, uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal eṉmaṉār pulavar- muṭipu
   vēṟupāṭukaḷai aṟintu naṭāttuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar; e-ṟu
   e-ṭu: "taṭavuttirai" eṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟun" taṭavu nilai"
   (puṟam-140) eṉa ukaram peṟṟum akara īṟṟu uriccol vantatu. atavattaṅ
   kaṉi eṉa vēṟṟumaikkaṇ akara īṟu attup peṟṟatu. kacatapat tōṉṟiṉ eṉa
   akara īṟṟiṉmuṉṉart takaraṅ koṭukka.
   "naṟavaṅ kaṇṇi naṟpōrc cempiyaṉ
   kurava nīṭiya koṉṟaiyaṅ ka.ṉal"
   eṉa ākāra iṟuti kuṟiyataṉiṟutic ciṉaikeṭṭu iruvaḻiyum ammup
   peṟṟaṉa.
   "muḷavumā tolaicciya painniṇap piḷavaip
   piṇavunāy muṭukkiya taṭiyoṭu viraii" (malaipaṭu-176)
   eṉa avvīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟāta muṭipupeṟṟaṉa. "tiṇ vaḷi vicitta
   muḻavo ṭākuḷi" (malaipaṭu-3) "cuṟaveṟimīṉ" "iravaḻaṅku ciṟuneṟi"
   (akam-318) ivai ukaram peṟāmal vantaṉa. "kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam
   poṟikkalai" (akam-97) eṉa ikara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammup peṟṟaṉa.
   "tīyiṉaṉṉa voṇceṅ kāntaḷ" (malaipaṭu-145) eṉa īkāra īṟu vēṟṟamaikkaṇ
   iṉpeṟṟatu. "nalloḻukkaṅ kākkun tiruvattavar" (nālaṭi-57) eṉa ukara īṟu
   vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attuppeṟṟatu. "ē*p peṟṟa māṉpiṇai pōla" (cintā-2965) eṉa
   ēkāra īṟu vēṟṟumaik kaṇ ekaram peṟātu vantatu. "kaittuṇṭām pōḻtē"
   (nālaṭi-19) 'kaittillar nallar' (nāṉmaṇikkaṭikai - 69) eṉavum,
   'puṉṉaiyaṅkāṉal' (akam - 80) 'mullaiyantoṭaiyal' eṉavum aikāra īṟu
   vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attum ammum peṟṟaṉa. 'aṇṇalkōyil vaṇṇamē' (cintā -
   nāmakaḷ- 126) eṉa ōkāra īṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟatu.
   iṉi 'añcevi1niṟaiyavāliṉa' (mullaip - 89) eṉa alvaḻikkaṭ kakaramum
   akaramuṅ keṭṭaṉa. 'maravam pāvai vayiṟārap paruki' 'maravanākam vaṇaṅki
   māṟkaṉam' eṉa iruvaḻiyum makaram vikārappaṭṭu ammup peṟṟaṉa. 'kāretir
   kāṉam pāṭiṉē māka' (puṟam - 144) 'poṉṉantikiri muṉ camatturuṭṭi'
   'poṉṉaṅ kuvaṭṭiṟ poliveyti' eṉa ṉakara īṟu iruvaḻiyum ammuppeṟṟaṉa.
   'vērpiṇi vetirattuk kālporu nālicai' (naṟṟi - 62) eṉa rakara īṟu
   vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attup peṟṟatu. 'nāvalan taṇpoḻil' (perumpāṇ - 465)
   'kāṉalam peruntuṟai' (aiṅkuṟu - 158) eṉa lakara īṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammup
   peṟṟaṉa. 'neytalañ ciṟupaṟai' iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu.
   'āyiṭaiyirupē rāṇmai ceyta pūcal' (kuṟun - 43) eṉpuḻi āyiṭaiyeṉpatu
   urupātaliṉ 'nīṭa varutal' (eḻu - 208) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyātu nīṇṭu vakara
   oṟṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu. temmuṉai eṉat tevveṉpuḻi vakaraṅkeṭṭu makara
   oṟṟup peṟṟu muṭintatu. a eṉṉuñ cuṭṭu 'aṉṟiyaṉaittum' eṉat tirintatu.
   'mutirkōṅkiṉ mukai' (kuṟiñcikkali - 20) eṉavuṅ 'kāymāṇṭa teṅkiṉ paḻam'
   (cintā - 31) eṉavuṅ kuṟṟukara īṟu iṉpeṟutaluṅ koḷka. 'toṇṭutalaiyiṭṭa
   pattukkuṟai' eṉa ṇakaram iraṭṭātu takara oṟṟu ṭakara oṟṟāyk
   kuṟṟiyalukaram ēṟi muṭintatu.2
   iṅṅañ ceyyuḷuṭ piṟavun tirivaṉa uḷavēṉum ip puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ muṭikka:
   arumaruntāṉeṉpatu rakaravukaraṅ keṭṭu arumantāṉeṉa muṭintatu. cōḻaṉāṭu
   cōṇāṭu eṉa aṉ keṭṭu muṭintatu. pāṇṭināṭum atu. toṇṭaimāṉāṭu toṇṭai
   nāṭeṉa īṟṟeḻuttuc cila keṭṭu muṭintatu. malaiyamāṉāṭu malāṭu eṉa
   mutaleḻuttoḻintaṉa palavuṅ keṭṭu muṭintatu. potuvi leṉpatu potiyileṉa
   ukaran tirintu ikaramāy yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu muṭintatu. piṟavum
   ivvāṟē tirintu maruvi vaḻaṅkuvaṉa ellām ip puṟaṉaṭaiyāṉ amaittukkoḷka.
   (78)
   ---
   1. akam + cevi = añcevi
       2. oṉpatu eṉṉuñ col toṇṭu eṉat tirintatu eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar
       karuttu. itaṉ tavaṟu muṉṉark kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.

   -------

              kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.
                     eḻuttatikāram muṟṟiṟṟu.

   nūliṉ marapu moḻimarapu nuṇpiṟappu
   mēlaip puṇarcci tokaimarapu - pālām
   urupiyaliṉ piṉṉuyir puḷḷi mayakkan
   terivariya kuṟṟukarañ ceppu.
   eḻuttati kārattuc cūttiraṅka ḷellām
   oḻukkiya oṉpatōt tuḷḷum - vaḻukkiṉṟi
   nāṉūṟ ṟirunāṟpāṉ mūṉṟeṉṟu nāvalarkaḷ
   mēṉūṟṟu vaittār virittu.
   --------------
                     ___________________________________

                             piṉṉiṇaippu.
                      2. kuṟṟiyalukaram uyirīṟē
                   (pulavar ñā. tēvanēyaṉār B.O.L.)

   "eḻutteṉappaṭupa,
   akaramutal ṉakaraiṟuvāy
   muppaḵteṉpa;
   cārntuvaral marapiṉ mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē." (tol. 1)
   "avaitām
   kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiya lukaram
   āytam eṉṟa
   muppāṟ puḷḷiyum eḻuttōraṉṉa." (tol. 2)
   eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
   iraṇṭām nūṟpāvil, kuṟṟiyalikaramum kuṟṟiyalukaramum āytam eṉṟa
   muppāṟpuḷḷiyum eṉap pirittuk kūṟātu "kuṟṟiyalikaram kuṟṟiyalukarma
   āytam eṉṟa muppāṟpuḷḷiyum" eṉṟu 'muppāṟ puḷḷi' eṉpatu mūveḻuttaiyum
   potuppaṭat taḻuvumāṟu ummait tokaiyāyc cērttuk kūṟiyiruttaliṉ,
   kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷum āytampōlap paṇṭaikkālattiṟ
   puḷḷipeṟṟaṉa eṉpatuṇarappaṭum.
   nūṟpāvil 'muppāṟpuḷḷi' yeṉpatu, puḷḷipeṟṟa mūveḻuttukkaḷaiyum muppuḷḷi
   vaṭivāṉa āytattaiyum oruṅkē kuṟippatu "āmākōṉav vaṇaiyavum peṟumē"
   eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāviṉ 'āmā' eṉpatupōṉṟa iraṭṭuṟalākum.
   tolkāppiya marapiyal nūṟpā 110-l, 'vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal' eṉṉum
   uttiyuraiyil,
   "kuṟṟiya likarattaip puḷḷi yeṉṟālum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅku
   nikaḻntaṉavākaliṉ avaiyum iṉi vārāmaiyāṉ vantuḻi avvāṟu āṇṭāṉeṉpatu.
   iṉip puḷḷiyeṉa mēl āḷa vārātataṉaip puḷḷi yeṉṟu āḷvaṉavaṟṟōṭu mayakkaṅ
   kūṟutaleṉpatu, 'avaitāṅ kuṟṟiyalikaraṅ kuṟṟiya lukara - māytameṉṟa -
   muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttōraṉṉa (tol - eḻut - nūṉ 2) eṉpuḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram
   puḷḷiyeṉṟu yāṇṭum āḷa vārāmaiyāṉum atutāṉ avvaḻi varavēṇṭutalāṉum
   aṅṅaṉam puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷavaruṅ kuṟṟukarattōṭum āytattōṭum uṭaṉ
   kūṟutalāyiṟṟu; iṅṅaṉam uṭaṉ kūṟākkāṟ puḷḷiyuṅ kuṟṟikaramumeṉac cūttiram
   peṟutal vēṇṭuvatāvāṉ cellumeṉpatu." eṉṟuraittār pērāciriyar.
   civañāṉa muṉivar tam tolkāppiya mutaṟ cūttiraviruttiyil,
   "oru moḻiyaic cārntuvaru miyalpaṉṟit taṉittiyaṅku miyalpu
   tamakkilaveṉṟaliṉ, avai tammaiyē yeṭuttōtik kāṭṭalākāmaiyiṉ, varuñ
   cūttirattāṉ avaṟṟiṟku vēṟuvēṟu peyariṭṭu 'avaitāṅ' - kuṟṟiya likaraṅ
   kuṟṟiyalukara - māytam' eṉṟum, am mūṉṟum puḷḷi peṟutal paṟṟip
   potuppeyarāka 'muppāṟpuḷḷiyum' eṉṟum, avai taṉit teḻutappaṭāvāyiṉum
   moḻiyoṭu cārtti yeḻutappaṭutaliṉ eḻutteṉṉuṅ kuṟiyīṭṭiṟkuriya veṉpār
   "eḻuttōraṉṉa" eṉṟum ōtiṉār." eṉṟār.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum "avaitām...eḻuttōraṉṉa" eṉṉum cārpeḻuttu
   nūṟpāvuraiyiṉ iṭaiyil,
   "āytameṉṟa ōcaitāṉ aṭuppukkūṭṭuppōla mūṉṟupuḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟeṉpatu
   uṇarttaṟku āytameṉṟa muppāṟpuḷḷiyumeṉṟār. ataṉai ikkālattār naṭuvu
   vāṅkiyiṭṭeḻutupa. itaṟku vaṭivukūṟiṉār, ēṉai yoṟṟukkaḷpōla uyirēṟātu
