   ōcai vikāramāy niṟpatoṉṟākaliṉ. eḻuttiyal taḻā ōcaikaḷ pōlak koḷḷiṉuṅ
   koḷḷaṟka eṉṟaṟku eḻuttēyāmeṉṟār. itaṉaip puḷḷivaṭiviṟṟeṉavē ēṉai
   yeḻuttukkaḷellām varivaṭiviṉavātal peṟṟām." eṉṟu kūṟiṉārēṉum
   toṭakkattil,
   "avaitām-mēṟcārntu varumeṉappaṭṭavaitām, kuṟṟiyalikaraṅ kuṟṟiyalukaram
   āytam eṉṟa muppāṟ puḷḷiyum-kuṟṟiyalikaramuṅ kuṟṟiyalukaramum
   āyatamumeṉṟu collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kūṟṟatākiya puḷḷi vaṭivumām;
   eḻuttōraṉṉa-avaiyum muṟkūṟiya muppateḻuttōṭu oru taṉmaiyāy vaḻaṅkum
   eṉṟavāṟu" eṉṟu mūveḻuttum puḷḷi peṟumeṉa orutaṉmaippaṭavē uraittār.
   mayilainātar "tollaivaṭiviṉa" eṉṉum naṉṉūl nūṟpāvuraiyil,
   "āṇṭu, eṉṟa mikaiyāṉē tātu, ētu eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattu āriya moḻikaḷum, eṭṭu,
   koṭṭu eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattup potu moḻikaḷum, kuṉṟiyātu, nāṭiyātu,
   eṭṭiyāṇṭuḷatu eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattup puṇarmoḻip poruḷvēṟupāṭukaḷum,
   aṟitaṟporuṭṭuk kuṟṟukarak kuṟṟikaraṅkaḷukku mēṟ puḷḷikoṭuppārum
   uḷareṉak koḷka." eṉṟu uraittaṉar.
   "kuṟṟiyalikaramuṅ kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkum; eṉṉai?
   ’kuṟṟiya likaramuṅ kuṟṟiya lukaramu
   maṟṟavai tāmē puḷḷi peṟumē’
   eṉpatu caṅkayāppākaliṉ" eṉpatu yāpparuṅ kalavirutti. (pakkam 27)
   mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl, kuṟṟiyalikara kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ paṇṭaikkālattil
   puḷḷiyiṭṭeḻutappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟum aṅṅaṉam eḻutiṉatu avaṟṟiṉ
   olikkuṟukkattaiyum "nāṭiyatu" "eṭṭiyāṇṭu" mutaliya puṇarmoḻip poruḷ
   vēṟupāṭṭaiyum kāṭṭaṟku eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
   ’nāṭiyatu’ ’eṭṭiyāṇṭu’ eṉpaṉa, nāṭi yātu eṭṭi āṇṭu, eṉṟum
   poruḷpaṭumātaliṉ avai nāṭu+yātu, eṭṭu+yāṇṭu eṉṉum puṇarmoḻikaḷ eṉṟu
   kāṭṭaṟkuk kuṟṟiyalikarattiṉ mēṟ puḷḷiyiṭappaṭṭateṉka.
   kuṟṟukarattai mēṟ cuṉṉamiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu iṉṟum malaiyāḷa (cēra) nāṭṭu
   vaḻakkam. kuṟṟikara kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ puḷḷi peṟumeṉṟu naṉṉūliṟ
   kūṟappaṭāmaiyāl 12-m nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē ivvaḻakku cōḻapāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷil
   oḻintatu eṉpatai aṟiyalām. ekara ēkāraṅkaṭṭum okara ōkāraṅkaṭkum avai
   cērntuḷḷa uyirmeykaṭkum varivēṟupāṭu tolkāppiyattilēyē
   kūṟappaṭṭiruppiṉum, 17-m nūṟṟāṇṭil avveḻuttukkaḷ kuṟil neṭil
   vēṟupāṭiṉṟi eḻutappaṭṭāṟpōla kuṟṟiyalukaramum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi
   yeḻutappaṭṭateṉka.
   oru poruḷ kuṟukiṉ apporuḷēyaṉṟi vēṟu poruḷākātu; ataṉ kuṟukkam aḷavu
   vēṟupāṭēyaṉṟip poruḷ vēṟupāṭaṉṟu, atupōl, kuṟṟiyalukaramum
   muṟṟiyalukaram pōla uyirēyaṉṟi meyyākātu. itaṉālēyē,
   "ikara ukaraṅ kuṟukiniṉṟaṉa, vikāravakaiyāṟ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutaliṉ,
   ivaṟṟai iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutal ellārkkum oppamuṭintatu. cantaṉakkōl
   kuṟukiṉāṟ pirappaṅkōlākātu. atupōla uyiratu kuṟukkamum uyirēyām.
   ivaṟṟaip puṇarcci vēṟṟumaiyum poruḷ vēṟṟumaiyum paṟṟi vēṟōr eḻuttāka
   vēṇṭiṉār" eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum. poruḷ vēṟṟumai yeṉṟatu,
   paṇṭaikkālattil kaṭṭu koṭṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷ ēval viṉaiyāmpōtu muṟṟukara
   vīṟāyum mutaṉilait toḻiṟ peyarāmpōtu kuṟṟukaravīṟāyum olikkappaṭṭamai
   nōkki. "tarukku aṇukku eṉpaṉa viṉaikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram." (tol.36,
   urai). "kātu kaṭṭu kattu murukku teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum
   kuṟṟukaramumāyp poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟāṟpōla" (tol.68, urai) eṉṟu
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka. tolkāppiyar, tam kālattil kuṟṟukaramum
   meypōlap puḷḷi peṟṟatiṉālēyayē, puṇariyalil,
   "meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal" (tol.104)
   "kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa" (tol.105)
   eṉṟu māṭṭēṟṟik kūṟiṉār.
   immāṭṭēṟṟattaik kavaṉiyātu, "kuṟṟiya...moḻipa," eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟku "īṟṟiṟ
   kuṟṟiyalukaramum (puḷḷiyīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkum) attaṉmaittu eṉṟu
   colluvar" eṉṟu iḷam pūraṇarum, "īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷiyīṟupōla
   uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar." eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum
   uraikūṟuvatu poruntātu. "aṟṟeṉa moḻipa" eṉṉum māṭṭēṟu "puḷḷiyoṭu
   nilaiyal" eṉpataiyē taḻuvumātaliṉ ivviruvarum iṅṅaṉam uraittaṟku, ivar
   kālattiṟkumuṉpē kuṟṟiyalukaram puḷḷipeṟum vaḻakku vīḻntamaiyē
   kāraṇamākum. ivar kūṟiya urai iṅkup poruntumāyiṉ, tolkāppiyar
   nūṉmarapil,
   "meyyi ṉiyaṟkai puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal” (tol.15)
   ”ekara okarat tiyaṟkaiyum aṟṟē" (tol.16)
   eṉṟu kūṟiyaviṭattum poruntavēṇṭum.
   ekara okaram puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭutta liṉmaiyāṉum,
   "ekara...aṟṟē" eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟku, "ekara okaraṅkaḷatu iyalpum avvāṟu
   puḷḷi peṟum iyalpiṟṟu," eṉṟu iḷampūraṇarum, "ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉatu
   nilaiyum meypōlap puḷḷi peṟum iyalpiṟṟu," eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum urai
   kūṟuvatāṉum, "kuṟṟiya.....moḻipa" eṉṉum nūṟpāviṟkum kuṟṟukaramum
   meypōlap puḷḷi peṟum eṉpatē uraiyākak kōṭal poruttamām.
   iḷampūraṇarum nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum, īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷi yīṟupōla
   uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukku meṉṟu uraittaviṭattum, māttiraik kuṟukkattāl
   aṅṅaṉam iṭaṅkoṭukkumeṉṉum karuttiṉarē yaṉṟi meyyīṟā yiruttalāl
   iṭaṅkoṭukkum eṉṉuṅ karuttiṉarallar. "kuṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vanta
   uyirēṟi muṭiya arai māttiraiyāy niṟṟalum, muṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vanta
   uyirēṟi muṭiyāmaiyun tammuḷ vēṟṟumai," (tol.36, urai) eṉṟu
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟutal kāṇka.
   iṉi, tolkāppiyar karuttai nōkkiṉ, avar kuṟṟiyalukarattai meyyīṟeṉṟu
   koṇṭār eṉṟu koḷḷutaṟku eḷḷaḷavum iṭamillai. avar,
   "kuṟṟiya lukara muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ
   muṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum. (tol. 67.)
   eṉak kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻimutal varumeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. nuntai eṉṉum colliṉ
   mutalil (tolkāppiyar karuttiṉpaṭi) uḷḷa kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟāyiṉ,
   accol nntai eṉṟu eḻutappaṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉa meḻutappaṭāmaiyum,
   kuṟṟiyalukarattait tolkāppiyar meyyeṉṟu koṇṭiruppārāyiṉ, atu,
   "paṉṉī ruyirum moḻimuta lākum." (tol. 59)
   "uyirmey allaṉa moḻimuta lākā." (tol. 60)
   eṉṟu avar kūṟiyavaṟṟōṭu muraṇutalum nōkkuka.
   mēlum, orē yukaram ōriṭattuk kuṟṟukaramākavum ōriṭattu muṟṟukaramākavum
   olikkappaṭum. aṅṅaṉam olikkappaṭupavaṟṟuḷ, cilavaṟṟiṟkuk kuṟṟukara
   muṟṟukarap poruḷ vēṟupāṭuṇṭu, cilavaṟṟiṟkillai.
   "muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāa
   tappeyar maruṅkiṉ nilaiyiya lāṉa" (tol. 68)
   eṉṉum nūṟpāvaiyum, ataṉ uraiyil,
   "kātu kaṭṭu kattu murukku teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāyp
   poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu niṉṟāṟpōla, nuntai yeṉṟu itaḻ kuvittu muṟṟak
   kūṟiyaviṭattum itaḻ kuviyāmaṟ kuṟaiyak kūṟiyaviṭattum oru poruḷē
   tantavāṟu kāṇka. nuntā yeṉpatō veṉiṉ, aḵtu itaḻ kuvittē kūṟavēṇṭutaliṟ
   kuṟṟukaramaṉṟu. iyaleṉṟataṉāṉ iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum iraṇṭiṟkum
   vēṟupāṭiṉṟeṉṟu koḷka" eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraittiruppataiyum nōkkuka.
   itaṉāl kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyīṟāyiṉ muṟṟiyalukaramum meyyīṟāy ukaram eṉṉum
   uyirē tamiḻukkillai yeṉṟu peṟappaṭutalum aṅṅaṉamiṉmaiyum aṟika.
   ceyyuḷiyalil iruvakai yukaramu moṉṟākak koṇṭē,
   "iruvakai yukaramō ṭiyaintavai variṉē
   nērpu niraiyu māku meṉpa. " (tol. cey. 4)
   eṉṟār tolkāppiyar.
   itaṉ uraiyil, "iruvakai yukara meṉpaṉa, kuṟṟukara muṟṟukaraṅkaḷ.
   avaṟṟōṭu mēṟkūṟiya nēracaiyum niraiyacaiyum orucolviḻukkāṭupaṭa
   iyaintuvariṉ niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē nēracaiyō ṭoṉṟivanta kuṟṟukaramum
   ataṉōṭu oṉṟivanta muṟṟukaramum nērpacai yeṉappaṭum; niraipacaiyō
   ṭoṉṟivanta kuṟṟukaramum ataṉōṭoṉṟivanta muṟṟukaramum niraipacai
   eṉappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu...
   "muṉṉar nēracai nāṉkum niraiyacai nāṉkumeṉa eṇ vakaiyāṉ acaikūṟi
   avaṟṟuppiṉ iruvakai yukaramum varumeṉavē, avai kuṟṟukarattōṭu eṭṭum
   muṟṟukarattōṭu eṭṭumākap patiṉāṟu utāraṇap pakutiyavāyc ceṉṟatēṉum
   avaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟeḻuttup piṉ varum ukaram nērpacaiyākā teṉpatu ’kuṟiliṇai
   yukara malvaḻi yāṉa’ (tol. cey. 5) eṉpuḻic collutum; oḻintaṉa kuṟṟukara
   nērpacai mūṉṟum niraipacai nāṉkumāyiṉa. utāraṇam: vaṇṭu, nāku, kāmpu;
   varaku, kuraṅku, malāṭu malāṭṭu; ivai kuṟṟukaram aṭuttu nērpum niraipum
   vantavāṟu" eṉṟu uraittār pērāciriyar. itaṉāl kuṟṟukaramum
   muṟṟukarampōla alaku peṟumeṉṟum acaik kuṟuppāmeṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum.
   coṟkaḷ ilakkaṇa muṟaiyilum ilakkiya muṟaiyilum palavakaiyākat tirintu
   muṉpu muṟṟukaravīṟā yiruntavai allatu ukaramallāta īṟā yiruntavai piṉpu
   kuṟṟukaravīṟākiṉṟaṉa. avvakaikaḷāvaṉa:-
   1. toḻiṟpeyar:- mutaṉilaitirinta toḻiṟpeyar:- e-ṭu: paṭu-
   pāṭu, cuṭu - cūṭu.
   vikutipeṟṟa toḻiṟ peyar:- e-ṭu: paṭi+pu=paṭippu, muṭi+cu=muṭiccu.
   2. veṟṟumaip peyar:- e-ṭu: yāṉ+ku=eṉakku, avar+ku= avarkku.
   3. kuṟippuviṉai muṟṟu:- e-ṭu: tāḷ+tu= tāṭṭu kaṇ+tu=kaṭṭu pāl+tu= pāṟṟu,
   aṉ+tu= aṟṟu.
   4. piṟaviṉai:- e-ṭu: paṭu+tu= paṭuttu, naṭa+tu=naṭattu, vāḻ+ tu=vāḻttu,
   pāy+cu=pāyccu.
   5. pōli:- e-ṭu: aṭaivu-aṭavu-aṭaku.
   6. col tiripu:- e-ṭu: tirum-tirumpu, porun-poruntu, uriñ - uriñcu, uṭaṉ
   (uṭal)-uṭam-uṭampu, paṇ-pāṇ-pāṭu, kuḷ-koḷ-kōḷ-kōṇ-kōṭu, oḷi-oḷir-oḷiṟu,
   pō-pōtu.
   mēṟkāṭṭiya pāṭu paṭippu eṉakku tāṭṭu paṭuttu mutaliya coṟkaḷ, kuṟṟukara
   vīṟṟai meyyīṟākak koḷḷiṉ, muṟaiyē pāṭ paṭipp eṉakk tāṭṭ paṭutt eṉṟa
   mutal vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉa vākavaṉṟō koḷḷal vēṇṭum! itu ettuṇaip
   pētaimaiyākum! mēlum, āṭṭu pāṭṭu kalakku viḷakku mutaliya
   piṟaviṉaikaḷum toḻiṟ peyarkaḷum mutaṉilaiyīṟṟu valiyiraṭṭiyum mutaṉilai
   yiṭaimeli valittum muṟaiyē āṭu pāṭu kalaṅku viḷaṅku eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟu
   tirintirukkavum, avaṟṟai āṭṭ pāṭṭ kalakk viḷakk eṉṉum vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu
   piṟantaṉavākak koḷḷal iḷañciṟārum eḷḷinakaiyāṭat takkatoṉṟaṉṟō? oru
   cila oliyaṭic coṟkaḷoḻinta ellāc coṟkaḷum ōracaiyāṉa vērc coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē
   piṟantavai yātaliṉ, kuṟṟiyalukarattai meyyīṟākak koḷvārellām
   coṟpiṟappiyalai eṭṭuṇaiyum tāmaṟiyātiruttalai muṟṟuṟak kāṭṭupavarē
   yāvar.
   kuṟṟukaravīṟu meyyīṟāyiṉ, aṟuvakaik kuṟṟiyalukarat toṭarkaḷum valliṉa
   meyyīṟṟaṉavātal vēṇṭum.
   "ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷa eṉṉum
   appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti." (tol. 78)
   eṉṟu tolkāppiyar pirinilaiyēkāraṅ koṭuttuk kūṟiṉamaiyāṉum,
   kuṟṟukaravīṟu tamiḻccollil ōriṭattum meyyīṟāka eḻutappaṭāmaiyāṉum,
   aḵtuṇmaiyaṉmai veḷḷiṭai malai.
   kaṇ+yātu = kaṇṇiyātu eṉpatu pōṉṟē cukku+yātu = cukkiyātu eṉṟu
   kuṟṟukaramum puṇarvatāl, kuṟṟiyalukarattai meyyīṟākak koḷḷalāmē yeṉiṉ,
   katavu+yātu=kataviyātu eṉṟu muṟṟukaramum aṅṅaṉam puṇarvatāl atuvum
   meyyīṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭu ukaravuyirē tamiḻukkillaiyeṉṟākum eṉṟu
   kūṟiviṭukka. tolkāppiyar moḻimarapil,
   "ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē."
   "uppakāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa." (tol. 75, 76.)
   eṉṟu varaiyaṟuttatu ucu mucu tapu eṉṉum mūṉṟu muṟṟukaravīṟṟuc
   coṟkaḷaiyē yaṉṟik kuṟṟukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷaiyaṉṟu. cu, pu, eṉṉum iru
   muṟṟukara īṟukaḷaikkoṇṭa coṟkaḷ ettaṉaiyeṉṟu varaiyaṟuppatē mēṟkūṟiya
   nūṟpākkaḷiṉ nōkkam. ku, ṭu, tu, ṟu eṉṉum nāṉku muṟṟukaravīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum
   aṟuvakaik kuṟṟukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum aḷaviṟantaṉa vātaliṉ, avaṟṟai
   varaiyaṟuttilar tolkāppiyar. iku uku ceku taku toku naku neku paku puku
   miku vaku viku eṉak kukaravīṟum, aṭu iṭu uṭu eṭu oṭu kaṭu keṭu koṭu
   cuṭu taṭu toṭu naṭu neṭu noṭu paṭu piṭu maṭu vaṭu viṭu eṉa ṭukaravīṟum
   koṇṭa muṟṟukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷ peruntokaiyiṉavāyum eṇ varampu
   paṭātaṉavāyu miruttal kāṇka. piṟa muṟṟukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷum iṅṅaṉamē.
   kuṟṟukara vīṟṟuc coṟkaḷō veṉiṉ akarātikaḷālum varaiyaṟukkappaṭāta
   pallāyirak kaṇakkiṉa.
   mēṟkūṟiya nūṟpākkaḷiṉ uraiyil,
   "ukārattōṭu kūṭiya cakāram iraṇṭumoḻikkē
   īṟām eṉṟavāṟu"
   "e-ṭu: 26, iḵtu uḷuviṉ peyar. mucu, itu kuraṅkiṉuḷ oru cāti. pacu
   eṉpatōveṉiṉ, aḵtu āriyac citaivu. kaccu kuccu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram.
   ukaram ēṟiya cakaram irumoḻikku īṟāmeṉavē ēṉai uyirkaḷ ēṟiya cakaram
   paṉmoḻikku īṟāmāyiṟṟu" (tol.75, urai) eṉṟum,
   "ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakaram orumoḻikkallatu paṉmoḻikku
   īṟākāteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar. "
   "eṭu; tapu eṉavarum,....uppu kappu eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram,
   ukarattōṭukūṭiya pakaram oṉṟeṉavē ēṉaiyuyirkaḷōṭukūṭiya pakaram
   paṉmoḻikku īṟāyp pala poruḷ tarumeṉṟārāyiṟṟu" (tol. 76 urai) eṉṟum
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiyiruttalai nōkkuka.
   itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl kuṟṟiyalukaram uyirīṟē yeṉṟum, tolkāppiyar
   ōriṭattum kuṟṟiyalukarattai meyyīṟākak koṇṭilareṉṟum, iyaṟkaiyum
   eḷimaiyum taṉimaiyum tāymaiyum cāṉṟa tamiḻiyalpai yaṟiyāta
   āriyavaḻiyayalārē tam moḻiyaip pōṉṟē tamiḻum āriya vaḻitteṉṟu mayaṅki,
   tamiḻai vaṭamoḻi vaḻittākak kāṭṭak karuti ataṟku mutaṟpaṭiyākak
   kuṟṟukaravīṟṟai meyyīṟākak kūṟi yiṭarppaṭṭup piṉpu avviṭarppāṭṭiṉiṉṟu
   nīṅkap paṇṭait tamiḻccoṟkaḷ valliṉa meyyilum iṟṟaṉa veṉṟum,
   valiyiraṭṭal eṉa oru tirivu muṟaiyum aravu eṉa oru toḻiṟpeyar vikutiyum
   illaiyeṉṟum, colliyal nūlukkum moḻi nūlukkum muṟṟum māṟākat tattam
   uḷantirintavāṟē uraippareṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
   ---------------------

   nacciṉārkiṉiyar iḷampūraṇaroṭu māṟupaṭum iṭaṅkaḷ

   1 ciṟappuppāyiram
   vaṭa vēṅkaṭan teṉkumari
   āyiṭait
   tamiḻkūṟu nallulakattu
   vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu māyiru mutaliṉ.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   (i-ḷ:) vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari a iṭait tamiḻ kūṟum nal ulakattu vaḻakkum
   ceyyuḷum a iru mutaliṉ- vaṭakkiṉkaṇ ṇuḷatākiya vēṅkaṭamum teṟkiṉkaṇ
   ṇuḷatākiya kumariyumākiya avaṟṟai ellaiyāka vuṭaiya nilattu vaḻaṅkum
   tamiḻmoḻiyiṉaik kūṟum naṉmakkaḷāṉ vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkum ceyyuḷumākiya iru
   kāraṇattāṉum.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   vaṭa vēṅkaṭan teṉkumari āyiṭai-vaṭakkiṉkaṇ vēṅkaṭamun teṟkiṉkaṭ
   kumariyumākiya avviraṇṭellaikkuḷḷiruntu, tamiḻkūṟum nal ulakattu
   vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum ā iru mutaliṉ-tamiḻaic collum nallāciriyaratu
   vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷumākiya avviraṇṭaiyum aṭiyākak koḷḷukaiyiṉālē.
   -----------
   2. nūṟpā 22
   ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅakiṉ
   meymmayak kuṭaṉilai teriyuṅ kālai.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   itu, taṉimey mayakkattiṟkup peyarum muṟaiyum tokaiyum uṇarttutal
   nutaliṟṟu.
   (i-ḷ:) a mū āṟum -mēṟ collappaṭṭa (mūvāṟu) patiṉeṭṭu meyyum, vaḻaṅku
   iyal maruṅkiṉ-tammai moḻippaṭutti vaḻaṅkum iyalpu uḷatāmiṭattu, mey
   mayakku-mey mayakkam eṉṟum, uṭaṉ nilai-uṭaṉilai mayakkam eṉṟum
   iruvakaiya, teriyum kālai-(avai mayaṅku muṟaimai) ārāyum kālattu.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   itu taṉimey piṟameyyoṭun taṉ meyyōṭun mayaṅkum mayakkamum uyirmey
   uyirmeyyoṭun taṉimeyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   (i-ḷ): ammūvāṟum-aṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu kūṟākap pakutta patiṉeṭṭu meyyum,
   vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ-vaḻakkiṭattuñ ceyyuḷiṭattum eḻuttukkaḷaik kūṭṭi
   moḻippaṭutta vaḻaṅkutal uḷatāmiṭattu, mey mayaṅkum nilai-taṉimey taṉ
   muṉṉar niṉṟa piṟameyyōṭun taṉmeyyōṭum mayaṅkum nilaiyum, uṭaṉ mayaṅkum
   nilai-appatiṉeṭṭum uyiruṭaṉē niṉṟu taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa uyirmeyyōṭun
   taṉimeyyōṭum mayaṅkum nilaiyumeṉa iraṇṭām, teriyuṅkālai-avai mayaṅkum
   moḻiyān taṉmai ārāyuṅkālattu e-ṟu.
   ------------
   3. 27
   ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
   yaḵkā ṉiṟṟiṉ meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   itu meymayakkam āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
   (i-ḷ): ñana mava eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar-ñanamava eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa
   puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar. yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal meypeṟṟaṉṟu-yakaram mayaṅki niṟṟal
   poruṇmai peṟṟatu ēkāram īṟṟacai.
   e-ṭu: uriñyātu porunyātu tirumyātu tevyātu eṉavarum.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar: (pak. 32)
   iṅṅaṉam āciriyar cūttirañ ceytaliṉ akkālattu oru moḻiyāka vaḻaṅkiya
   coṟkaḷ uḷaveṉpatu peṟṟām. avai ikkālattu iṟantaṉa.
   iṉi uraiyāciriyar uriñyātu porunyātu tirumyātu tevyātu eṉa irumoḻikkaṇ
   varuvaṉa utāraṇamākak kāṭṭiṉārā leṉiṉ, āciriyar oru moḻiyāmāṟu īṇṭuk
   kūṟi, irumoḻi puṇarttaṟkup puṇariyaleṉṟu vēṟōr iyaluṅkūṟi, ataṉkaṇ
   "meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ mey varu vaḻiyum" eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. kūṟiyavaḻip piṉṉum
   "ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyiṟuti" eṉṟum piṟāṇṭum īṟukaṭōṟum eṭuttōtip
   puṇarppar. ātaliṉ īṇṭu irumoḻip puṇarcci kāṭṭiṟ kūṟiyatu kūṟa leṉṉuṅ
   kuṟṟamām. ataṉāl avai kāṭṭutal poruntāmai uṇarka.
   4. 34
   kuṟṟiya likara niṟṟal vēṇṭum
   yāveṉ ciṉaimicai yuraiyacaik kiḷavik
   kāvayiṉ varūu makara mūrntē.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   (i-ḷ:) kuṟṟiyalikaram-oru moḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram, uraiyacaik
   kiḷavikku-uraiyacaic collākiya miyā eṉ mutaṟku, āvayiṉ
   varūum-(ciṉaiyāka) accoṟṟaṉṉiṭattu varukiṉṟa, yāeṉ ciṉaimicai-yā eṉ
   ciṉaimicai, makaram ūrntu niṟṟal vēṇṭum-makara oṟṟiṉai ūrntu niṟṟalai
   vēṇṭum (āciriyaṉ.)
   e-ṭu: kēṇmiyā eṉavarum. miyā eṉṉum col iṭam. makaram paṟṟukkōṭu.
   yāeṉṉum ciṉaiyum makarampōlak kuṟukutaṟku oru cārpu.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   (i-ḷ) uraiyacaik kiḷavikku varūum-tāṉ kūṟum poruḷaik kōṭaṟku oruvaṉai
   etirmukamākkum colliṟkup poruntavarum, āvayiṉ-am miyāveṉṉum
   iṭaiccollaic collumiṭattu, yāveṉ ciṉaimicai makaram ūrntu- yāveṉṉum
   uṟuppiṉ mēlatāy mutalāy niṉṟa makaravoṟṟiṉai yēṟi, kuṟṟiyalikaram
   niṟṟal vēṇṭum-kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟalai virumpum āciriyaṉ e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kēṇmiyā ceṉmiyā eṉavarum, kēḷeṉṟatu uraiyacaik kiḷavi; ataṉaic
   cārntu taṉakku iyalpiṉṟi niṉṟatu miyā veṉṉum iṭaiccol. avviṭaiccol
   mutalum ataṉiṟ piriyum yā ataṟku uṟuppu māmeṉṟu karuti yāveṉ ciṉai
   yeṉṟār. miyā iṭam; makaram paṟṟukkōṭu. yāvum ikaram araimāttirai
   yātaṟkuc cārpu.
   ------------
   5. 40.
   uruviṉu micaiyiṉu marukit tōṉṟu
   moḻikkuṟip pellā meḻutti ṉiyalā
   āyta maḵkāk kālai yāṉa.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   (i-ḷ:) uruviṉum-oru poruḷiṉatu uruvattiṉkaṇṇum, icaiyiṉum-ōcaiyiṉ
   kaṇṇum, arukit tōṉṟum- ciṟupāṉmaiyāyt tōṉṟum; kuṟippu moḻi yellām-
   kuṟippu moḻikaḷellām, eḻuttiṉ iyalā-āyta eḻuttāṉiṭṭu eḻutappaṭṭu
   naṭavā. (aḵtu ekkālattumō veṉiṉ, aṉṟu.) āytam aḵkā kālaiyāṉa-avvāytam
   taṉ arai māttirai aḷapāyc curuṅki nillātu (avvuruvum icaiyatu mikutiyum
   uṇarttutaṟku) nīṇṭa kālattu annīṭcikku.
   e-ṭu: "kaḵṟeṉṟatu" eṉpatu uruvu. "cuḵṟeṉṟatu" eṉpatu icai.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   (i-ḷ.) uruviṉum icaiyiṉum arukit tōṉṟum kuṟippu moḻiyum-niṟattiṉkaṇṇum
   ōcaiyiṉkaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmai āytantōṉṟum poruḷ kuṟittalaiyuṭaiya collum,
   ellā moḻiyum-avai yoḻinta ellā moḻikaḷum, eḻuttiṉiyalā-oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ
   pōla arai māttiraiyiṉ kaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmai mikkum naṭantu, āytam aḵkāk
   kālaiyāṉa-āytañ curuṅkāta iṭattāṉa coṟkaḷām e-ṟu.
   eṉavē...peṟṟām
   eḻuttiṉeṉṟa...viṉaiyeccam.
   ivvāṟaṉṟi ikkuṟippuc coṟkaḷ āytam iraṇṭiṭṭu eḻutappaṭāveṉṟu poruḷkūṟiṟ
   ceyyuḷiyalōṭu māṟupaṭṭu māṟukoḷak kūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟantaṅkumeṉṟu uṇarka.
   6. 45
   ōreḻut torumoḻi yīreḻut torumoḻi
   iraṇṭiṟan ticaikkun toṭarmoḻi yuḷappaṭa
   mūṉṟē moḻinilai tōṉṟiya neṟiyē.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   e-ṭu: ā-ōreḻut torumoḻi maṇi-īreḻut torumoḻi varaku, koṟṟaṉ-mūveḻut
   torumoḻi
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   e-ṭu: ā, kā, nā-ōreḻut torumoḻi
   maṇi varaku koṟṟaṉ-īreḻut torumoḻi
   kurava arava-mūveḻut torumoḻi
   kaṇaviri-nāleḻut torumoḻi
   akattiyaṉār-ainteḻut torumoḻi
   tirucciṟṟampalam-āṟeḻut torumoḻi
   perumpaṟṟappuliyūr-ēḻeḻut torumoḻi
   kuṟippu: nacciṉārkkiṉiyar oṟṟaiyum kuṟṟukarattaiyum eṇṇavillai.
   -------------
   7. 47
   tammiyal kiḷappi ṉellā veḻuttu
   meynnilai mayakka māṉa millai.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   itu, meym mayakkattiṟku ōr puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
   (i-ḷ: ellā eḻuttum tam iyal kiḷappiṉ-ellā meyyeḻuttum moḻiyiṭai yiṉṟit
   tam vaṭiviṉ iyalpaic collumiṭattu, meymayakka nilai māṉam illai-meym
   mayakka nilaiyiṉ mayaṅki varutal kuṟṟam illai.
   e-ṭu: "valleḻuttiyaiyiṉ ṭakāramākum" eṉavarum.
   itaṉai ammeymmayakkattu vaikka veṉiṉ, itu vaḻuvamaiti nōkki
   moḻimarapiṉkaṇṇa tāyiṟṟu.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   itu muṉṉar meykkaṇ uyir niṉṟavāṟu kūṟi avvuyir meykkaṇ ēṟi uyirmeyyāy
   niṉṟakālattu ammeyyāṟpeyar peṟumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
   (i-ḷ: ellā eḻuttum -paṉṉīruyirum, meynnilai tam iyal mayakkaṅ
   kiḷappiṉ-meyyiṉ taṉmaiyilē tammuṭaiya taṉmai mayaṅkiṟṟākap peyar kūṟiṉ,
   māṉamillai-kuṟṟamillai e-ṟu.
   meyyiṉ taṉmaiyāvatu...........kūṟiṉār.
   iḵtaṉṟip patiṉeṭṭu meyyiṉ taṉmai kūṟumiṭattu meymmayakkam kūṟiya
   vakaiyāṉaṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu mayaṅkumeṉṟu kūṟi "avaṟṟuḷ laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar"
   eṉpataṉaikkāṭṭil aḵtu irumoḻik kaṇṇateṉa maṟukka.
   ---------
   8. 48
   yaraḻa veṉṉu mūṉṟumuṉ ṉoṟṟak
   kacatapa ṅañanama vīroṟ ṟākum.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   (i-ḷ) yaraḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟu-yaraḻa eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟu,
   muṉoṟṟa -(kuṟiṟkīḻum neṭiṟkīḻum) muṉṉē oṟṟāy niṟpa (avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉē)
   kacatapa ṅa ña na ma īr oṟṟākum-ka ca ta pa kkaḷiloṉṟātal ṅa ña na
   makkaḷiṉoṟṟātal oṟṟāy vara avai īroṟṟuṭaṉilaiyām.
   e-ṭu: vēykkuṟai, vēyṅkuṟai, vērkkuṟai, vērṅkuṟai vīḻkkuṟai vīḻṅkuṟai,
   ciṟai, talai, puṟam eṉa oṭṭuka.
   ivviti mēl īṟṟakattu uṇarntukoḷḷap paṭumāleṉaṉ, itu "īrkku" "pīrkku"
   eṉaorumoḻiyuḷ varutalāṉum iraṇṭu moḻikkaṇ varutal vikāramātalāṉum īṇṭuk
   kūṟappaṭṭatu. aḵtēl, itaṉai nūṉmarapiṉakattu meymmayak kattukkaṇ
   kūṟukaveṉiṉ, āṇṭu vēṟṟumai nayam koṇṭatākaliṉ mūvoṟṟu uṭaṉilaiyātal
   nōkki oṟṟumai nayampaṟṟi īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   e - ṭu : vēykka vāycci pāyttal vāyppu eṉavum, pīrkku nērcci vārttal
   ārppu eṉavum, vāḻkkai tāḻcci tāḻttal tāḻppu eṉavum, kāyṅkaṉi tēyñcatu
   kāyntaṉam kāympuṟam eṉavum, nērṅkal nērñcilai nērntilai nērmpuṟam
   eṉavum varum. ḻakārattiṟku vāḻntaṉam eṉa ikkālattu nakara voṟṟu varum.
   ēṉaiya mūṉṟum ikkālattu vaḻaṅkumeṉiṉ uṇarka.
   iṉit tāḻṅkulai tāḻñciṉai tāḻntiraḷ vīḻmpaṭai eṉa akkālattu vaḻaṅkumeṉṟu
   ittokaic coṟkaḷ kāṭṭalum oṉṟu. uraiyāciriyarum irumoḻikkaṭ
   kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiṟku avvīṟukaṭōṟum kūṟukiṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷ piṉṉar
   vēṇṭāmai uṇarka. iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉilai yātaliṉ īṇṭu vaittār.
   -----------
   9. 50
   kuṟumaiyu neṭumaiyu maḷaviṟ kōṭaliṟ
   ṟoṭarmoḻi yellā neṭṭeḻut tiyala
   iḷampūraṇar:
   (i-ḷ) kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum aḷaviṉ kōṭaliṉ - uyireḻuttiṟkuk kuṟumaiyum
   neṭumaiyum aḷaviṟ koḷḷappaṭutalil, toṭarmoḻikkīḻ niṉṟa rakāra
   ḻakāraṅkeḷḷalām neṭiṟkīḻ niṉṟa rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpaiyuṭaiya (eṉṟu
   koḷḷappaṭum).
   e-ṭu : akar, pukar, akaḻ, pukaḻ eṉak koḷka.
   'pulavar' eṉṟāṟpōla iraṇṭu māttiraiyai iṟantataṉ piṉṉum varumāleṉiṉ,
   avaiyum "taṉṉiṉa muṭittal" eṉpataṉāl 'neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟu' eṉappaṭum. ellām
   eṉṟataṉāṉ, rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷēyaṉṟi, piṟa oṟṟukkaḷum 'neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟu'
   eṉappaṭum. itaṉāṉē, viral tītu eṉpuḻi lakaram 'neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟu' eṉṟu
   keṭukkappaṭum.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   (i - ḷ.) kuṟumaiyum neṭumaiyum - eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟiya taṉmaiyum neṭiya
   taṉmaiyum, aḷaviṟ kōṭaliṉ - māttirai yeṉṉum uṟuppiṉaic cevi karuviyāka
   aḷakkiṉṟa aḷavu toḻilālē ceyyuṭkuk koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ, toṭarmoḻi yellām-am
   māttirai tammuḷ toṭarntuniṟkiṉṟa coṟkaḷellām, neṭṭeḻuttiyala-neṭṭeḻuttu
   māttirai mikku naṭakkumpaṭiyākat toṭarnta collām e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: "varuvarkol vayaṅkiḻā ay" eṉavum "kaṭiyavē kaṉaṅkuḻā ay" eṉavuṅ
   kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām neṭṭeḻuttiṉai māttirai mikuttaṟkuk kūṭiyavāṟu
   uṇarka. ēṉaicceyyuṭkaḷaiyum ivvāṟē kāṇka. eṉavē, māttirai aḷakkuṅkāl
   neṭṭeḻuttē māttiraipeṟṟu mikku niṟkum eṉaṟamaiyāṉ, etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum
   uttipaṟṟic ceyyuḷiyalai nōkkik kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. īṇṭuk kūṟiṉār, neṭṭeḻuttu
   iraṇṭu māttiraiyiṉ ikantu varumeṉpataṟivittaṟku.
   ........iṉi uraiyāciriyar pukar pukaḻ eṉak kuṟiliṇaik kīḻ rakāra
   ḻakāraṅkaḷ vanta toṭarmoḻika ḷellām tār tāḻ eṉṟāṟpōla ōcaiyottu
   neṭṭeḻuttiṉ taṉmaiyavām eṉṟārāleṉiṉ, pukar pukaḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟai
   neṭṭeḻutteṉṟē evviṭattum āḷāmaiyāṉum neṭṭeḻuttākak kūṟiya ilakkaṇattāl
   oru payaṉ koḷḷāmaiyāṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaluḷ ivaṟṟaik kuṟiliṇai oṟṟaṭutta
   niraiyacaiyākavum tār tāḻ eṉapaṉavaṟṟai neṭṭeḻuttu oṟṟaṭutta
   nēracaiyākavum kōṭalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmai uṇarka.
   -----------
   10. 53
   moḻippaṭut ticaippiṉum torintuvēṟicaippiṉum
   eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉmaṉār pulavar.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   itu eḻuttukkaṭku moḻikkaṇ māttirai kāraṇamākap piṟappatōr aiyam tīrttal
   nutaliṟṟu.
   (i-ḷ) moḻippaṭuttu icaippiṉum-moḻikkaṇ paṭuttuc colliṉum, terintu vēṟu
   icaippiṉum-terintu koṇṭu vēṟē colliṉum, eḻuttu iyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār
   pulavar-uyirum meyyumākiya eḻuttukkaḷ (perukkam curukkam uṭaiyaṉapōṉṟu
   icaippiṉum) tattam māttirai iyalpil tiriyā eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.
   e-ṭu: aḵkal a eṉavum; āl, ā eṉavum; kaṭal, ka eṉavum; kāl, kā eṉavum
   kaṇṭukakoḷka.
   vēṟu eṉṟataṉāṉ eṭuttal paṭuttal mutaliya ōcai vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
   eḻuttiyal tiriyāveṉpatu koḷka.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   (i-ḷ: terintu-oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷ taru nilaimaiyai ārāyntu,
   moḻippaṭuttu icaippiṉum-collākac kōttuc colliṉum, vēṟu
   icaippiṉum-ceyyuḷiyaluḷ oṟṟuṅ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷtarumēṉum māttirai
   kuṟaintu niṟkum nilaimaiyai nōkki eḻutteṇṇappaṭā veṉṟu āṇṭaikku vēṟākak
   kūṟiṉum, eḻuttiyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar-avviraṇṭiṭattum arai māttirai
   peṟṟu niṟkum kuṟṟukaramum muṟkūṟiya eḻuttān taṉmai tiriyāveṉṟu kūṟuvar
   pulavar e-ṟu
   e-ṭu; al il uṇ eṇ ol eṉavum kal vil muḷcel eṉavum āl īr ūr ēr ōr eṉavum
   kāl cīr cūl tēṉkōṉ eṉavum uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya kaṟṟeḻuttaiyum
   neṭṭeḻuttaiyum oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷ tantavāṟu kāṇka. kaṭam
   kaṭām uṭaiyāṉ tiruvārūr akattiyaṉār eṉa īreḻuttaiyum mūveḻuttaiyum
   nāleḻuttaiyum aiyeḻuttaiyum iṟutiyilum iṭaiyilum oṟṟaṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷ
   tantavāṟu kāṇka. eḵku teḷku kokku kuraṅku eṉpaṉavum eḻutteṇṇavum
   alakiṭavum peṟāta kuṟṟukaram aṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷ tantavāṟu kāṇka.
   iṉi ic cūttirattiṟku eḻuttukkaḷaic collākkik kūṟiṉum. piṟitākak kūṟiṉum
   māttirai tiriyāteṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, akaram eṉpuḻiyum a eṉpuḻiyum ālam
   eṉpuḻiyum ā eṉpuḻiyum kakaram eṉpuḻiyum ka eṉpuḻiyum kālam eṉpuḻiyum kā
   eṉpuḻiyum ōcai ottu niṟkumeṉṟāl atu muṉṉark kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāṟ
   peṟappaṭutaliṟ payaṉil kūṟṟāmeṉka.
   -----------------
   11. 83
   ellā veḻuttuñ colluṅkālaip
   iḷampūraṇar:
   (i-ḷ: ellā eḻuttum neṟippaṭa nāṭi colluṅ kālai-tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷellām
   oruvaṉ muṟaippaṭa ārāyntu tammaic colluṅ kālattu.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   (i-ḷ) ellā eḻuttum piṟappiṉ ākkam colluṅ kālai-tamiḻeḻuttu
   ellāvaṟṟiṟkum āciriyaṉ kūṟiya piṟappiṉatu tōṟṟaravai yāṅ kūṟumiṭattu.

   12 111.
   maruviṉ ṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyum
   uriyavai yuḷavē puṇarnilaic cuṭṭē.
   112. iḷampūraṇar:
   (i-ḷ) maruviṉ tokuti mayaṅku iyal moḻiyum- (ilakkaṇa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi)
   marūuttiraḷākiya talai taṭumāṟāka mayaṅkiṉa iyalpaiyuṭaiya
   ilakkaṇattōṭu porunti marūu vaḻakkum, uriyavai uḷa puṇarnilaic
   cuṭṭu-uriyaṉa uḷa puṇarum
   nilaimaikkaṇ.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   (i - ḷ) marumoḻiyum - iruvakaiyākiya maruviya coṟkaḷum, iṉṟokuti
   mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyum - cevikkiṉitākac coṟṟiraḷiṭattu niṟutta colluṅ
   kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟu poruḷuṇarttātu pirintu
   piṉṉarc ceṉṟu oṭṭip poruḷuṇartta mayaṅkutal iyaṉṟa coṟkaḷum,
   puṇarnilaic cuṭṭu uriyavai uḷa - puṇarum nilaimaik karuttiṉkaṇ uriyaṉa
   uḷa, e - ṟu.
   -------------
   13. 210
   ceymmaṉa veṉṉun toḻiliṟu collum
   ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōṭ kiḷaviyum
   iḷampūraṇar:
   'uraiyiṟ kōṭal' eṉpataṉāl, viyaṅkōḷmuṉ vaikkaṟpālataṉai muṉvaiyātu
   ceymamaṉa eṉpataṉai muṉ vaittataṉāṉ, iv viyalpu muṭipiṉkaṇ ceymamaṉa
   eṉpatu ciṟappuṭaitteṉap peṟappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl ēval kaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷum iv
   viyalpu muṭipu uṭaitteṉak koḷka. atu, "maṉṉiya peruma nī" eṉa varum.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   e-ṭu : ..ēval kaṇṇiya eṉpataṉāṉ ēval kaṇṇātatum uḷateṉṟu kūṟi
   'maṉṉiyaperuma nī' eṉa utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭukaveṉiṉ, atu poruntātu;
   kūṟukiṉṟāṉa avaṉ nilaipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟē karutik kūṟutaliṉ atuvum
   ēval kaṇṇiṟṟēyām.
   ---------
   14. 233
   āṉmuṉ varūu mīkāra pakaram
   tāṉmikat tōṉṟik kuṟukalu murittē.
   iḷampūraṇar:
   (i - ḷ.) āṉmuṉ varum īkāra pakaram āṉ eṉṉum colmuṉṉar varumoḻiyāy
   varukiṉṟa īkārattoṭu kūṭiya pakaramākiya moḻi, tāṉ mikat tōṉṟi
   kuṟukalum urittu - appakaramākiya tāṉ mikattōṉṟi av vīkāram ikaramākak
   kuṟuki muṭitalaiyum uṭaittu.
   'tōṉṟi' eṉṟataṉāl, nilaimoḻippēṟākiya ṉakaravoṟṟiṉ kēṭu koḷka.
   nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   (i-ḷ.) āṉ muṉ varūum īkāra pakaram – āṉeṉṉuñ coṉmuṉṉar varumoḻiyāy
   varukiṉṟa īkārattōṭu kūṭiya pakaramākiya moḻi, tāṉ mikat
   tōṉṟi-appakaramākiya tāṉ mikku niṟpa nilaimoḻi ṉakarattiṟkuk kēṭu
   tōṉṟi, kuṟukalum urittu - īkāram ikaramākak kuṟuki niṟṟalum urittu e -
   ṟu.
   -----------
   15. 316.
   ilameṉ kiḷavikkup paṭuvaru kālai
   nilaiyalu murittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.
   317. iḷampūraṇar:
   (i-ḷ.) ilam eṉ kiḷavikku paṭu varu kālai - ilam eṉṉum colliṟkup paṭu
   eṉṉuñ col varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nilaiyalum urittu ceyyuḷāṉ - muṉ
   'makaraviṟuti' eṉpataṉāṟ keṭṭa īṟu keṭātu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittuc
   ceyyuṭkaṇ.
   e-ṭu: "ilampaṭu pulava rēṟṟakai niṟaiya" eṉa varum.
   uriccollākalāṉ urupu viriyāteṉiṉum ilattāṟ paṟṟappaṭum pulavareṉṉum
   poruḷ uṇara niṟṟaliṉ, vēṟṟumai muṭipāyiṟṟu. ummai makaravīṟu eṉṉum cāti
   yorumai paṟṟi vanta etirmaṟai.
   316. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   itaṉai ilattāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉa vēṟṟumai yeṉṟārāl
   uraiyāciriyareṉiṉ, paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉpatu peyareccamātaliṟ
   paṟṟaveṉṉun toḻil tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟa mutaṉilaic collaic cūttirattu āciriyar
   eṭuttōtiṟṟilarātalāṉum paṭuveṉpatu tāṉum pulavareṉṉum peyarōṭu
   muṭiyuṅkāl iraṇṭu kālamum kāṭṭum īṟukaḷ tokka mutaṉilaic collāy
   niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉai eṭuttōtiṉārātalāṉum āciriyarkku aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟutal
   karuttaṉmai uṇarka.
   aṉṟiyum paṟṟappaṭṭa eṉpuḻi iraṇṭu mutaṉilai kūṭi oṉṟāy niṉṟu
   paṟṟutalaic ceyyappaṭṭa eṉap poruḷ tārāmaiyāṉum alvaḻi
   yatikāramātalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmai uṇarka.
   409 iḷampūraṇar:
   allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
   ellā viṟutiyu mukara niṟaiyum.
   iḵtu eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
   (i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉum - alvaḻiyaic collum iṭattum, vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
   - vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇṇum, ellā iṟuti ukaramum niṟaiyum. āṟu
   īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum niṟaintē niṟkum.
   e-ṭu: nāku kaṭitu, nāku kaṭumai; varaku kaṭitu, varaku kaṭumai eṉa
   varum.
   408. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum
   ellā iṟutiyum ukara nilaiyum.
   iḵtu 'iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟuka miṭaṉu māṟuṇṭē' eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarmoḻikkaṇ
   araimāttiraiyiṉuṅ kuṟakumeṉa eytiyataṉai vilakki 'avviya ṉilaiyu mēṉai
   mūṉṟē' eṉṟa vitiyē peṟumeṉkiṉṟatu.
   (i-ḷ.) allatu kiḷappiṉum - alvaḻiyaic collumiṭattum, vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum -
   vēṟṟumaip puṇarccik kaṇṇum, ellā iṟutiyum ukaram nilaiyum - āṟu
   īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum ukaram taṉ arai māttiraiyaip peṟṟu niṟkum e - ṟu.
   -----
   17. 409.
   valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
   tollai yiyaṟkai nilaiyalu murittē.
   410. iḷampūraṇar:
   iḵtu eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.
   (i - ḷ.) valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi - (avavīṟṟuḷḷum) valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi,
   valleḻuttu varuvaḻi – valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi (varumoḻiyāy) varum iṭattu,
   tollai iyaṟkai nilaiyilum urittu - muṉ (kūṟiya) iyaṟkai niṟṟalum
   urittu.
   e-ṭu: kokkuk kaṭitu, kokkuk kaṭumai eṉa varum.
   409. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar:
   itu muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ arai māttirai peṟum eṉṟataṉai vilakki
   'iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟuku miṭaṉum' eṉṟataṉāṉ araimāttiraiyiṉum kuṟukumeṉṟu
   āṇṭu vitittatu īṇṭu valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇē varumeṉkiṉṟatu.
   (i-ḷ.) valloṟṟu toṭarmoḻi - valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi kuṟṟukaram, valleḻuttu
   varuvaḻi - valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, tollai iyaṟkai
   nilaiyalum urittu - 'iṭaippaṭiṟ kuṟukum’ eṉpataṉāṟ kūṟiya arai
   māttiraiyiṉum kuṟuki niṟkum eṉṟa iyalpilē niṟṟalum urittu e-ṟu.
   e-ṭu: kokkuk kaṭitu kokkuk kaṭumai eṉa araimāttiraiyiṟ kuṟaintavāṟu
   kuraṅku kaṭiteṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu paṭuttuc cevikaruviyāka uṇarka.
   muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattu ukara niṟaiyumeṉṟu pāṭam ōti ataṟku ukaram arai
   māttiraiyiṟ ciṟitu mikku niṟkumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi ic cūttirattiṟkup
   paḻaiya arai māttirai peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar.
   ------
